**[00:31:28.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000067)  
The consultation of the Committee on Finance, Subcommittee A, with civil society organizations to discuss budgetary priorities for the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget is hereby called to order. I'd like to acknowledge the presence of our colleague, Senator Panfilo Lacson.

**[00:31:48.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000068)  
And before we recognize the resource persons, the Chair would like to state some guidelines for our orderly conduct for this afternoon. Due to the limited number of seats available at the panel, only one resource person per CSO will be seated at the panel. Should a question be addressed or answered by your colleague, you must first seek the permission of the Chair to have your colleague recognized before they may respond.

**[00:32:14.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000069)  
We would like also to remind everyone that each CSO is allotted a maximum of five minutes to deliver its statement or presentation. A timer will be displayed on the screen to help everyone keep track of their allotted time. Thank you for your understanding and cooperation. We welcome our partners from civil society organizations and all our guests for today's discussions. At this juncture, may I request the Committee Secretary to acknowledge the resource persons present for today.

**[00:36:12.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000077)  
Thank you, Comsec. Magandang araw po sa ating lahat, lalo na sa ating mga kasamahan sa Senado at sa ating mga katuwang mula sa civil society organizations. Every peso we allocate represents a choice about the kind of future we want for our people. These choices must be informed by the experiences of the communities we serve. This is why your participation in the budget process matters.

**[00:36:35.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000078)  
Noong nakaraang taon, nagsagawa ng konsultasyon sa mga civil society organizations ang ating Senate President Sherwin Gatchalian, na noon ay chairperson ng Committee on Finance. We recognize the foundation he laid for greater public participation in our budget deliberations. Under our chairmanship, we kept building on that effort by bringing your participation directly into the budget hearings.

**[00:37:02.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000079)  
Sinulatan natin ang mga vice chairperson ng Committee on Finance upang mabigyan ng puwang ang mga CSO sa mga pagdinig ng kanilang mga subcommittee. May ilang vice chairperson natin na nag-imbita ng mga CSO at iba pang grupo upang makaupo sa mga hearing at maibahagi ang kanilang mga pananaw sa mga panukalang budget ng mga ahensya.

**[00:37:27.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000080)  
We also invited you to submit questions that we could raise on your behalf. Sinama natin ang inyong mga tanong at alalahanin sa pagbusisi ng budget ng bawat ahensya. Ipinadala rin natin ang mga ito sa mga kinauukulang departamento upang mabigyan ng kaukulang pansin, sagot at aksyon. We want your participation to have a meaningful place in the scrutiny of the proposed budgets of our national government

**[00:37:55.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000081)  
agencies, in the questions officials are asked to answer and in the priorities we put forward. Mahalaga ang inyong ambag dahil marami sa inyo ang direktang nakikipag-ugnayan sa mga komunidad. Your experience helps us to achieve our goal of going back to basics. As we prepare our 2027 budget, you help us assess whether the proposed allocations respond to the actual needs of the people.

**[00:38:22.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000082)  
Today, we are also working to make budget information more accessible and useful to the public. Kasalukuyan nating isinusulong ang pagsasama sa Senate Budget Transparency Portal ng ilan sa mga tools ng ating mga katuwang, kabilang ang People's Budget Coalition at Better Gov PH. Through this effort, we hope to bring these tools to a wider audience and help more Filipinos understand, examine and monitor the national budget. Transparency is more felt when people can actually use the information to ask questions and hold their government accountable.

**[00:38:58.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000083)  
We're applying the same commitment to transparency in our scrutiny of the proposed DPWH budget na tumaas ng higit P11.49 billion sa ilalim ng General Appropriations Bill. Mula sa 11,420 projects under the 2027 NEP, lumaki rin po ang kabuuang bilang ng mga proyekto sa 16,244 sa ilalim ng GAB.

**[00:39:22.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000084)  
Kapansin-pansin din po sa aming pagsusuri ang paglobo ng pondo sa ilalim ng DPWH Convergence Program tulad ng mga multipurpose buildings at basic infrastructure projects na umabot sa P74 billion. Samantala, binawasan ng higit P73 billion ang pondo para sa malalaking foreign-assisted projects na importante sa pagpapalago ng ating ekonomiya. Ito rin ang dahilan kung kaya bumuo tayo ng isang dedicated na team na nakatutok lamang sa masusing line-by-line review ng NEP at General Appropriations Bill mula sa Kamara.

**[00:39:58.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000085)  
Sinuri po natin ang mga pagbabago sa nakalaang pondo at bilang ng mga proyekto, ang kanilang mga supporting documents at kung may repetition o pag-uulit sa mga proyektong may nakalaang pondo sa kasalukuyang budget. Our initial review has flagged about P266 billion worth of projects for further scrutiny and review, including concerns over the supporting documents submitted to the Committee.

**[00:40:25.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000086)  
Of the 16,244 total projects under the DPWH operations budget, only 1,418 projects, or 8.73%, were submitted with accompanying physical or digital documentation. A staggering 14,826 projects, or 91.27%, had no submitted supporting documents.

**[00:40:52.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000087)  
Kasama rin po dito ang 23 line items na may laang pondo sa ilalim ng 2026 General Appropriations Act na may kabuuang halaga ng P227.2 million. We also note several lump-sum line items worth P20.5 billion under the GAB. These findings require clear explanations from the DPWH at mahalaga na bago tayo maglaan ng pondo, malinaw ang batayan, pangangailangan at dokumentasyon ng bawat proyekto.

**[00:41:25.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000088)  
Beyond this year's budget review, we want these reforms to endure beyond one's chairmanship, Senate Committee on Finance or budget cycle. Kaya po ngayong araw, bago ang pagbubukas ng konsultasyon na ito kasama ang civil society organizations at government watchdogs, naghahain tayo ng tatlong panukalang batas upang mapagtibay at gawing mas makabuluhan ang pakikilahok ng ating mga mamamayan sa pagbabalangkas, pamamahala at paglalaan ng pondo ng bayan.

**[00:41:59.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000089)  
First, the Bantay Budget Act or Senate Bill No. 2576, which seeks to institutionalize CSO participation in the preparation and authorization of the budget. It provides avenues for you to submit proposals, participate in budget hearings and access budget documents, including records of bicameral conference proceedings. Second, the Grassroots Participatory Budgeting Act or Senate Bill No. 2577 seeks

**[00:42:29.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000090)  
to bring community-identified priorities into local and national budgeting.

**[00:54:02.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000390)

**[00:54:15.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000396)

**[00:54:21.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000399)

**[00:54:23.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000400)

**[00:54:27.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000402)

**[00:54:29.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000403)

**[00:54:33.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000405)

**[00:54:36.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000406)

**[00:54:43.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000410)

**[00:54:45.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000411)

**[00:54:48.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000412)

**[00:54:50.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000413)

**[00:54:54.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000415)

**[00:54:56.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000416)

**[00:54:58.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000417)

**[00:55:00.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000418)

**[00:55:04.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000420)

**[00:55:08.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000422)

**[00:55:10.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000423)

**[00:55:11.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000424)

**[00:55:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000425)

**[00:55:19.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000428)

**[00:55:22.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000429)

**[00:55:24.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000430)

**[00:55:26.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000431)

**[00:55:27.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000432)

**[00:55:30.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000433)  
Likewise, I just like to

**[00:55:32.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000434)  
reiterate

**[00:55:34.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000435)  
the priorities

**[00:55:36.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000436)  
I would like as Chair

**[00:55:38.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000437)  
of the Committee on Finance.

**[00:55:40.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000438)  
Dalawang bagay. Una-una

**[00:55:42.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000439)  
infrastructure development. I've been

**[00:55:44.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000440)

**[00:55:46.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000441)  
kahit na nasa House of Representatives

**[00:55:48.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000442)  
because I believe that

**[00:55:49.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000443)  
with infrastructure development, we will lay

**[00:55:52.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000444)  
down the

**[00:55:54.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000445)  
we need to lay down the infrastructure.

**[00:55:56.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000446)  
It will stimulate economic growth,

**[00:55:57.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000447)  
spread out the development, and

**[00:56:00.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000448)  
create jobs and opportunities.

**[00:56:02.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000449)  
Lagi kong sinasabi, although

**[00:56:04.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000450)  
kailangan natin yung mga social programs

**[00:56:06.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000451)  
and ayuda, that is not

**[00:56:08.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000452)  
sustainable. Kailangan natin yan

**[00:56:10.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000453)  
sa crisis. Kailangan natin may

**[00:56:11.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000454)  
El Nino, but it has to be a balance.

**[00:56:14.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000455)  
Hindi natin pwedeng pabayaan

**[00:56:15.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000456)

**[00:56:17.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000457)  
infrastructure projects, that will really

**[00:56:19.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000458)  
be

**[00:56:21.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000459)  
the one that will give

**[00:56:24.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000460)  
jobs and opportunities.

**[00:56:26.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000461)  
Especially in the future. Ito magbibigay

**[00:56:27.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000462)  
ng tunay na trabaho, tahan na buhay.

**[00:56:30.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000463)  
And that's what we have to work on.

**[00:56:32.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000464)  
Hindi natin dapat. Kaya nabahala ako

**[00:56:33.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000465)  
nung natanggal yan yung

**[00:56:34.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000466)  
foreign assisted.

**[00:56:37.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000467)  
Sabi ko, hindi pwedeng

**[00:56:39.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000468)  
I will not as

**[00:56:41.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000469)  
Chair of the Committee on Finance,

**[00:56:43.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000470)  
I will make sure that the

**[00:56:45.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000471)  
infrastructure projects, lalo yung foreign assisted,

**[00:56:47.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000472)  
will be continuous. Malayo-layo

**[00:56:50.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000473)  
ang ating haabulin sa ating

**[00:56:51.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000474)  
ASEAN neighbors.

**[00:56:54.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000475)  
Ito'y madi-delay pa. Talagang

**[00:56:55.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000476)

**[00:56:57.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000477)  
Foreign investments are going

**[00:56:59.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000478)  
to Indonesia, to Malaysia.

**[00:57:02.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000479)  
Vietnam is

**[00:57:03.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000480)  
getting triple of what we're getting.

**[00:57:06.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000481)  
So kailangan natin humabol.

**[00:57:08.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000482)  
So I will really prioritize

**[00:57:09.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000483)  
infrastructure development.

**[00:57:11.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000484)  
And likewise, of course, another

**[00:57:13.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000485)  
issue close to my heart in our

**[00:57:15.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000486)

**[00:57:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000487)  
Ito ramdam talaga ng taong bayat.

**[00:57:19.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000488)  
Kaya sana ito po yung ating

**[00:57:21.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000489)

**[00:57:24.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000490)  
nung si Senator

**[00:57:25.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000491)  
Gatchalian,

**[00:57:26.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000492)

**[00:57:29.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000493)

**[00:57:32.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000494)  
Pero nung hindi ko

**[00:57:33.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000495)

**[00:57:36.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000496)

**[00:57:37.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000497)

**[00:57:39.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000498)  
Pero okay lang. Sa tulong ninyo,

**[00:57:42.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000499)

**[00:57:43.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000500)

**[00:57:45.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000501)

**[00:57:47.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000502)  
Pero nandyan na rin lang yan.

**[00:57:49.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000503)  
Sabi naman yan, sinabi ni Senator Gatchalian,

**[00:57:51.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000504)

**[00:57:53.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000505)  
it's a position of trust. Wala akong makikita.

**[00:57:55.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000506)  
Na

**[00:57:56.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000507)  
maisip.

**[00:57:58.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000508)  
Siyempre, ano naman ako.

**[00:58:00.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000509)  
Siyempre, I took that as a challenge.

**[00:58:03.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000510)  
So I'm hoping that

**[00:58:04.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000511)  
with you there, we're hoping that

**[00:58:07.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000512)  
the reforms will continue.

**[00:58:10.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000513)  
And we will be able to bring back

**[00:58:11.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000514)  
the trust in the budget process.

**[00:58:13.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000515)  
Okay? So again, thank you very much.

**[00:58:15.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000516)  
We will start the ball rolling.

**[00:58:18.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000517)  
First on the list is

**[00:58:19.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000518)  
People's Budget Coalition.

**[00:58:21.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000519)  
Our co-convener,

**[00:58:22.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000520)  
Kenneth Abante.

**[00:58:24.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000521)  
You are now recognized.

**[00:58:26.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000522)  
Thank you, Chair JV.

**[00:58:28.520] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000523)  
Magandang hapon po, SP Win,

**[00:58:30.460] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000524)  
Senator Ping. May we ask

**[00:58:32.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000525)

**[00:58:35.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000526)  
we will have a team

**[00:58:36.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000527)

**[00:58:38.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000528)

**[00:58:40.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000529)  
Yes, TJ Montesa.

**[00:58:43.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000530)  
You're recognized.

**[00:58:47.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000531)  
Yes, magandang hapon po,

**[00:58:48.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000532)  
Chair JV, SP Win, and Senator Ping.

**[00:58:51.580] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000533)  
Ako po si AJ Montesa

**[00:58:52.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000534)  
from the People's Budget Coalition.

**[00:58:54.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000535)  
Lampas isang trilyon pesos po

**[00:58:56.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000536)  
ang red flags na nakita namin

**[00:58:58.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000537)  
sa 2027 budget.

**[00:59:00.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000538)  
210 billion pesos sa soft pork

**[00:59:02.580] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000539)  
sa anyon ng patronage-driven

**[00:59:04.540] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000540)  
ayuda. 695 billion

**[00:59:07.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000541)  
sa hard pork, which are

**[00:59:08.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000542)  
politicized infrastructure.

**[00:59:10.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000543)  
And 112 billion pesos po sa

**[00:59:12.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000544)  
shadow pork, o yung unprogrammed appropriations.

**[00:59:15.460] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000545)  
In our assessment,

**[00:59:16.440] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000546)  
altogether, these produce a budget that serves

**[00:59:18.540] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000547)  
politicians before it serves the people.

**[00:59:21.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000548)  
BBC's National Coordinator,

**[00:59:23.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000549)  
Abigail Absmodalis, should,

**[00:59:24.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000550)  
be the one speaking here in my place today.

**[00:59:27.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000551)  
But Abs's mother was recently diagnosed

**[00:59:28.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000552)  
with cancer. And when she

**[00:59:30.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000553)  
brought her mother home from the emergency room

**[00:59:32.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000554)  
last October 11 with a 77,000

**[00:59:34.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000555)  
peso bill for chemotherapy

**[00:59:36.520] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000556)  
complications, PhilHealth

**[00:59:38.580] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000557)  
covered only 11,000 pesos.

**[00:59:40.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000558)  
So she did what thousands of

**[00:59:42.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000559)  
Filipinos do every day.

**[00:59:44.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000560)  
She wrote to politicians' offices

**[00:59:45.980] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000561)  
asking for a guarantee letter

**[00:59:48.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000562)  
but came away with nothing.

**[00:59:50.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000563)  
This is the system that the House of Representatives

**[00:59:52.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000564)  
chooses to feed.

**[00:59:54.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000565)  
Public money for medical and crisis assistance

**[00:59:56.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000566)  
that reaches the people if the right

**[00:59:59.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000567)  
politician and the right people

**[01:00:00.010] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000568)  
The National Expenditure Program proposed an amount of P72 billion for these ayuda lines.

**[01:00:06.980] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000569)  
AICS, MAIFIP, TUPAD, Presidential Assistance for Farmers and Fisherfolk, Tulong Dunong, worth P72 billion.

**[01:00:15.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000570)  
The House of Representatives gives this P137 billion, nearly double for ayuda.

**[01:00:21.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000571)  
Year after year, the House tends to bloat these programs that give preferential treatment to beneficiaries through letters that legislators hold,

**[01:00:30.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000572)  
lodged in the budgets of DSWD, DOH, and DOLE, which implicitly carve out these funds for the individual legislators.

**[01:00:38.880] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000573)  
Bukot pa dito, binawasan pa po ng Kamara ang health promotion, immunization, and health facilities upang madagdagan ang budget ng MAIFIP.

**[01:00:46.960] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000574)  
So it seems that the House wants us to get more sick so that we can beg more for our hospital bills.

**[01:00:53.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000575)  
So based on this, we respectfully ask that the Senate help us advance four reforms to address soft pork.

**[01:00:58.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000576)  
Una, ilipan.

**[01:01:00.000] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000577)  
Bukot po ang pondo mula sa pabor, mula patungo sa karapatan.

**[01:01:04.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000578)  
Realign the House's ayuda increases to the rules-based programs.

**[01:01:07.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000579)  
For example, from MAIFIP to PhilHealth, from Tulong Dunong to Tertiary Education Subsidy, and from AICS to 4Ps.

**[01:01:15.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000580)  
Pangalawa po, tanggalin ang guarantee letter at referral letter system, pati ang mga nakatagong alokasyon para sa mga politiko.

**[01:01:22.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000581)  
So write into the special provisions of AICS, MAIFIP, TUPAD, and PAFF

**[01:01:26.380] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000582)  
that referrals and guarantee letters come only from the implementing agency

**[01:01:30.360] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000584)  
Or hospital social services, never from legislators and their staff.

**[01:01:34.880] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000585)  
And to ban allocations reserved for individual legislators with penalties for violations.

**[01:01:40.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000586)  
Pangatlo po, set clear processing timelines, priority lanes for medical and funeral emergencies, alternatives for people without complete documents or PhilSys ID,

**[01:01:50.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000587)  
and a public appeals process so people know where to apply and how long they'll wait and to contest a denial.

**[01:01:54.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000588)  
At pang-apat, panatilihing bukas ang pinto gaya ng ginagawa natin po ngayon.

**[01:02:00.360] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000589)  
Make CSO participation a regular, written part of the Senate's budget process

**[01:02:05.000] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000590)  
because we are the ones who live with the effects of every decision in the national budget.

**[01:02:10.080] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-000591)  
Salamat po, Mr. Chair. I'll turn it over po to my colleague, Ms. Ziza Suzara, with your permission.

**[01:02:14.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000592)  
Thank you, AJ.

**[01:02:16.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000593)  
Tiga, please.

**[01:02:24.920] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000594)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Ziza.

**[01:02:26.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000595)  
Ziza.

**[01:02:27.400] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000596)  
Yes. Good afternoon, Ms. Ziza.

**[01:02:29.480] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000613)  
Senate President Win Gatchalian and Senator Ping Lacson. We in the People's Budget Coalition welcome the landmark ruling of the Supreme Court on the unprogrammed appropriations. But we cannot move forward without accountability. The use of the unprogrammed appropriations has been shrouded in opacity since 2022. Based on our analysis, P499 billion was certified by the Treasury as available for the UA in 2024 to 2025 alone.

**[01:03:03.700] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000625)  
Half a trillion pesos, controversially drawn from PhilHealth, PDIC, and other GOCC funds

**[01:03:11.720] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000626)  
as authorized under the now unconstitutional special provision 1D of the 2024 UA.

**[01:03:19.500] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000627)  
Hindi pa kasama ang mga ginamit na pondo sa 2022 at 2023.

**[01:03:25.620] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000628)  
Out of the 499 billion,

**[01:03:29.460] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000629)  
393 billion in total was released to different agencies.

**[01:03:34.940] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000630)  
No one knows how these funds were actually expended.

**[01:03:39.820] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000631)  
40% or 195 billion of the 493 billion was released to DPWH.

**[01:03:49.440] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000632)  
107 billion of that went to flood control.

**[01:03:53.540] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000633)  
Pagkatapos ng flood control scan,

**[01:03:55.620] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000634)  
papatanong ka, may substandard at ghost projects rin?

**[01:03:59.460] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000635)  
Kaya sa mga ito?

**[01:04:01.180] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000636)  
Furthermore, 29 billion was released to DSWD for AICS.

**[01:04:06.540] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000637)  
10 billion was released to DOH for MAEFIP.

**[01:04:10.260] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000638)  
Tulad ng LGS, lalong-lalo na ang Falgo.

**[01:04:14.160] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000639)  
Extra hard pork at soft pork rin pala ang naging priority sa unprogrammed appropriations.

**[01:04:21.280] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000640)  
The bloating of the UA and the cuts in the FAPs over the past fiscal years

**[01:04:27.280] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000641)  
are two sides of the...

**[01:04:29.460] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000642)  
of the same coin.

**[01:04:30.780] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000643)  
As the UA became a dumping ground for deprioritized FAPs.

**[01:04:35.420] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000644)  
Compared to the insertions that replaced them in the HGAB,

**[01:04:39.600] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000645)  
FAPs undergo a lengthy process of technical scrutiny and approval.

**[01:04:44.180] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000646)  
They must remain in the programmed appropriations.

**[01:04:48.600] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000647)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:04:49.740] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000648)  
Senate President,

**[01:04:50.760] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000649)  
Senator Lacson,

**[01:04:51.840] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000650)  
isa lang ang pinupuntahan ng mga analysis ng PBC sa UA, FAPs at LGSF.

**[01:04:58.820] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000651)  
The National...

**[01:04:59.460] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000652)  
The national budget is undeniably captured by patronage politics.

**[01:05:04.620] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000653)  
So much so that the fiscal program gets extremely distorted every single year since 2022.

**[01:05:12.900] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000654)  
This corrosive budget politics has eroded overall budget credibility and public trust.

**[01:05:20.500] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000655)  
It is us, taxpayers, who bear the consequences of these fiscally irresponsible choices.

**[01:05:27.460] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000656)  
This should end.

**[01:05:29.460] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000657)  
We call on the Senate to recast the 2027 budget into a genuinely people-centered budget.

**[01:05:36.420] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000658)  
And we urge the Senate as well to conduct a full inquiry on the use of the 2022 to the 2025 unprogrammed appropriations.

**[01:05:46.660] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000659)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:05:47.920] People's Budget Coalition Representative Zy-za Nadine Suzara** (asr-000660)  
I turn it over to Ken Abante.

**[01:05:51.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000661)  
Thank you, Ziza.

**[01:05:53.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000662)  
Ken?

**[01:05:57.720] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000663)  
Maraming salamat po, Chair.

**[01:05:59.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000664)  
Finally, we would like to flag billions of pesos in hard pork or infrastructure program.

**[01:06:05.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000665)  
Sabi nga po namin sa mga nakaraang hearing,

**[01:06:09.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000666)  
ang warning po namin sa publiko ay posibleng magkaroon ulit tayo ng flood control scandal sa ibang ahensya

**[01:06:15.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000667)  
if we allow the House budget to pass as is.

**[01:06:18.480] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000668)  
This is the same modus operandi of the insertions of Speaker Martin Romualdes and Congressman Zaldico

**[01:06:23.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000669)  
na nagdala po sa atin sa sitwasyong ito.

**[01:06:25.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000670)  
Nag-insert ng proyektong walang batayang dokumento.

**[01:06:29.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000671)  
Heto po ang updated partial and unofficial results ng aming risk analysis of infrastructure budgets as of October 26, 2026.

**[01:06:38.420] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000672)  
Halos 7,000 projects worth 190 billion pesos ang in-insert ng House na walang published project attachments.

**[01:06:47.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000673)  
In the DPWH, the House inserted nearly 6,200 projects worth 172 billion.

**[01:06:53.420] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000674)  
Many of them are multi-purpose buildings.

**[01:06:55.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000675)  
In the DOH, the House inserted more than 180 projects.

**[01:06:59.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000676)  
New health facilities worth more than 2 billion pesos, but they cut the overall health facilities budget.

**[01:07:05.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000677)  
In the Department of Agriculture, the House inserted more than 350 new farm-to-market roads worth nearly 6 billion pesos,

**[01:07:13.880] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000678)  
with some coordinates in the Benham Rise, in Celebesi, and some buildings are even in Algeria and Nigeria.

**[01:07:23.260] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000679)  
In the National Irrigation Administration, the House inserted more than 200 new irrigation projects,

**[01:07:29.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000680)  
worth more than 7 billion pesos, with some cost assumptions that are questionable.

**[01:07:33.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000681)  
We note, Mr. Chair, that this does not yet include the master list of allocations from the Department of Education schools to check

**[01:07:42.140] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000682)  
so that ordinary citizens and media can check whether the places with the highest shortages are really the ones getting the funds.

**[01:07:50.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000683)  
Hindi po namin sinasabi na katiwalian ang lahat ng ito.

**[01:07:52.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000684)  
Kailangan po natin ng infrastrukturang ito.

**[01:07:55.420] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000685)  
Pero sana maayos.

**[01:07:56.580] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000686)  
At ang panawagan po namin,

**[01:07:59.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000687)  
kung walang published na dokumento,

**[01:08:01.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000688)  
tanggalin ng Senado.

**[01:08:03.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000689)  
I-vito ng Pangulo.

**[01:08:05.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000690)  
Sa budget na pinaghirapang buwis ng taong bayan,

**[01:08:08.540] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000691)  
bawal magkatay po.

**[01:08:10.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000692)  
Ang worry po namin,

**[01:08:11.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000693)  
hindi pa rin po natututo o nahiya ang kamera sa kabila ng lahat ng pagmamarcha at rally namin.

**[01:08:17.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000694)  
Sa kabila ng sigaw ng taong bayan,

**[01:08:18.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000695)  
hindi pa rin natauhan.

**[01:08:20.640] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000696)  
But we are thankful that our honorable senators are listening to our calls for systemic reforms.

**[01:08:25.380] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000697)  
Night and day po talaga yung pakiramdam naming budget advocates.

**[01:08:29.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000698)  
Kaya po ngayon, inilulunsad namin ang Budget Insertions Tracker sa budget.bettergov.ph

**[01:08:37.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000699)  
so that every taxpayer, every voter, every Filipino can be empowered to demand

**[01:08:42.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000700)  
that their legislators explain insertions in their districts.

**[01:08:45.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000701)  
Dito po nakasaad ang top districts with the biggest allocations,

**[01:08:49.580] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000702)  
with the biggest insertions, even with the biggest cuts.

**[01:08:52.380] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000703)  
You can click on every district and see all the 18,000 projects,

**[01:08:59.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000705)  
Including the 7,000 projects that they inserted.

**[01:09:04.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000706)  
We incorporated these over the weekend.

**[01:09:06.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000707)  
At ang nakita po namin na may pinakamalalaking budget in the projects that the House added,

**[01:09:11.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000708)  
Ilocos Norte, 1st District, 4.375 billion.

**[01:09:15.720] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000709)  
Misamis Occidental, 2nd District, 1.8 billion.

**[01:09:19.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000710)  
Davao de Oro, 2nd District, 1.7 billion.

**[01:09:22.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000711)  
Isabela, 4th District Office, 1.7 billion.

**[01:09:25.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000712)  
South Cotabato, 2nd District Office, 1.6 billion.

**[01:09:29.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000713)  
Bohol, 1st District, 1.5 billion.

**[01:09:32.180] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000714)  
Nueva Ecija, 1st District, 1.3 billion.

**[01:09:35.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000715)  
Patanga, 2nd District, 1.3 billion.

**[01:09:37.940] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000716)  
Zambale, 2nd District, 1.2 billion pesos.

**[01:09:41.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000717)  
Ito po ang mga district engineering offices.

**[01:09:44.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000718)  
We need to reimagine our people power in the budget.

**[01:09:48.020] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000719)  
To prevent the cancer of corruption from spreading,

**[01:09:50.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000720)  
we need to strengthen our nation's immune system.

**[01:09:52.880] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000721)  
That means all of us, media, civil society, academe, and oversight agencies

**[01:09:57.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000722)  
have the data to demonstrate the power of the budget.

**[01:09:58.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000723)  
To demand accountability from the people who decide on our taxpayer money.

**[01:10:04.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000724)  
Muli po, buwis natin to, boto natin to, at budget natin to.

**[01:10:09.480] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-000725)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:10:11.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000726)  
Okay, thank you.

**[01:10:12.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000727)  
Next to present is from GoodGov PH.

**[01:10:17.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000728)  
Presented by Mr. Michel Andre Macahilo.

**[01:10:21.820] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000729)  
Good afternoon to everyone.

**[01:10:23.720] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000730)  
To the Honorable Chair J.V. Ejercito,

**[01:10:25.580] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000731)  
to the Senate President, Wyn Gatchalian,

**[01:10:27.240] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000732)  
and to the Vice Chairperson,

**[01:10:28.840] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000733)  
Senator Panfilo Lacson,

**[01:10:30.400] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000734)  
we thank the Honorable Committee

**[01:10:31.560] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000735)  
in giving us space and the opportunity

**[01:10:33.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000736)  
to convey our position specific to the youth

**[01:10:35.660] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000737)  
in asks with regards to certain programs

**[01:10:37.760] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000738)  
in the 2027 budget.

**[01:10:39.540] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000739)  
We'd like to zero in on the DepEd Youth Formation Division,

**[01:10:42.880] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000740)  
more specifically with their programs

**[01:10:44.780] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000741)  
like the Learner Government Program,

**[01:10:46.640] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000742)  
Career Guidance Program, etc., etc.

**[01:10:49.060] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000743)  
What is the problem?

**[01:10:50.320] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000744)  
Based on our analysis and in the current budget framework,

**[01:10:53.340] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000745)  
the LGP and several other programs

**[01:10:55.240] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000746)  
such as the Career Guidance Program,

**[01:10:56.820] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000747)  
the Learners Formators Enhancement,

**[01:10:58.840] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000748)  
Enhancement Program, and etc.

**[01:11:00.600] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000749)  
remain part of the discretionary funds

**[01:11:02.760] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000750)  
of the Bureau of Learner Support Services.

**[01:11:04.760] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000751)  
Therefore, it remains unclear

**[01:11:06.760] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000752)  
whether the program will receive enough funding

**[01:11:09.040] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000753)  
to ensure meaningful participation,

**[01:11:11.040] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000754)  
capacity building for Supreme Elementary Learner Governments

**[01:11:14.480] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000755)  
and likewise Supreme Secondary Learner Governments.

**[01:11:17.280] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000756)  
Additionally,

**[01:11:18.240] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000757)  
P250 million was removed from the budget of the Bureau

**[01:11:21.800] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000758)  
when the NEP was passed.

**[01:11:23.000] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000759)  
And the second part of the problem is just

**[01:11:25.440] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000760)  
public M&E documents specific to the LGP and other

**[01:11:28.640] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000761)  
adjacent programs are not recognized.

**[01:11:30.520] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000762)  
And we also based on our analysis

**[01:11:32.680] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000763)  
that only some regional offices published data about the LGP.

**[01:11:36.360] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000764)  
But even then, the published data is either

**[01:11:38.640] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000765)  
for certain component programs of the LGP such as elections,

**[01:11:42.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000766)  
so it's incomplete at the end of the day.

**[01:11:44.440] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000767)  
Or in existing documents, it's oversimplified

**[01:11:47.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000768)  
or it comes in passing.

**[01:11:48.480] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000769)  
Therefore, these programs are heavily insulated

**[01:11:50.720] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000770)  
from public scrutiny.

**[01:11:52.280] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000771)  
Also, recent incidents of school-based violence

**[01:11:55.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000772)  
across the country highlight the need for avenues for healthy,

**[01:11:58.320] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000773)  
socialization, instilling of positive values

**[01:12:01.840] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000774)  
and cultivation of love of community and country.

**[01:12:04.680] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000775)  
We respectfully ask, therefore,

**[01:12:06.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000776)  
the Senate Committee on Finance for these specific measures.

**[01:12:09.600] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000777)  
First, it's to increase DepEd, BLS, and YFD budget

**[01:12:13.480] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000778)  
by at least P250 million.

**[01:12:15.440] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000779)  
This is so the Bureau can fund the following.

**[01:12:17.640] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000780)  
The institutionalization of after-school programs,

**[01:12:20.200] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000781)  
clubs, and organizations so that the DepEd can also do things

**[01:12:23.600] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000782)  
like review of the policy framework

**[01:12:25.200] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000783)  
on the after-school co-curricular and extracurricular programs.

**[01:12:28.320] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000784)  
Secondly, it's to strengthen learner governments

**[01:12:31.120] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000785)  
as a civic education engagement platform.

**[01:12:33.600] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000786)  
That is to say, they will do things like consultations,

**[01:12:35.960] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000787)  
policy discussions, and policy validation.

**[01:12:38.440] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000788)  
Third is to strengthen the career guidance program

**[01:12:41.280] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000789)  
and ensure these programs are written as separate line items

**[01:12:44.640] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000790)  
to guarantee appropriate funding for these programs

**[01:12:49.000] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000791)  
so that they are able to be scrutinized

**[01:12:50.840] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000792)  
in cases where budget's insufficient.

**[01:12:53.120] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000793)  
To quote EDCOM 2's report on the Filipino learner today,

**[01:12:56.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000794)  
when adequately...

**[01:12:58.320] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000795)  
When adequately supported,

**[01:12:59.480] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000796)  
this becomes programs, laboratories rather,

**[01:13:02.120] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000797)  
of participation where students deliberate on shared issues,

**[01:13:05.320] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000798)  
organize initiatives, and experience responsibility

**[01:13:07.840] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000799)  
for collective outcomes.

**[01:13:09.280] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000800)  
Education for citizenship as articulated in young people

**[01:13:12.280] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000801)  
learning to become active citizens is most effective

**[01:13:15.120] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000802)  
when it allows the youth to learn, participate,

**[01:13:17.080] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000803)  
and reflect and lead within communities

**[01:13:18.760] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000804)  
that value their voice.

**[01:13:20.080] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000805)  
We therefore urge the Senate to act on these measures

**[01:13:22.520] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000806)  
for the Filipino youth,

**[01:13:23.720] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000807)  
and to ensure that their learning is holistic

**[01:13:25.600] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000808)  
and goes beyond the classroom,

**[01:13:27.120] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000809)  
which allows them to have access

**[01:13:28.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000810)  
to pathways of sustainable outlets and alternatives

**[01:13:30.960] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000811)  
compared to the prevailing culture now

**[01:13:32.880] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000812)  
where they are nudged to isolation

**[01:13:34.280] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000813)  
because of a lack of access to these programs.

**[01:13:36.600] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000814)  
Learner governments and school-based organizations

**[01:13:39.120] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000815)  
offer young people opportunities

**[01:13:40.920] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000816)  
to kickstart their respective leadership journeys.

**[01:13:42.960] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000817)  
Learner governments and clubs can train people in leadership,

**[01:13:45.920] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000818)  
civic engagement, and advocacy in their purpose,

**[01:13:48.880] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000819)  
which are crucial for leading youth governance institutions

**[01:13:51.840] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000820)  
in preparing for public leadership.

**[01:13:53.800] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000821)  
Again, from GoodGov PH,

**[01:13:56.000] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000822)  
maraming salamat at mabuhay ang kabataang Pilipino.

**[01:13:58.160] GoodGovPH Representative Michel Andre D. Macahilo** (asr-000823)  
Thank you.

**[01:13:59.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000824)  
Thank you.

**[01:14:00.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000825)  
Mr. Michelle Macahilo, next.

**[01:14:03.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000826)  
On the list is Social Watch Philippines

**[01:14:06.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000827)  
and NCPAG-UP,

**[01:14:08.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000828)  
Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza,

**[01:14:10.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000829)  
co-convenor of Social Watch.

**[01:14:14.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000830)  
You have the floor.

**[01:14:15.640] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000831)  
Thank you.

**[01:14:17.200] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000832)  
Good afternoon.

**[01:14:18.640] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000833)  
On behalf of Social Watch Philippines,

**[01:14:21.320] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000834)  
I will present our assessment of the proposed

**[01:14:23.560] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000835)  
2027 Budget to the Lens of Social Equity and Human Rights.

**[01:14:28.160] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000836)  
My colleagues around the table will subsequently elaborate

**[01:14:32.760] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000837)  
on our findings and recommendations.

**[01:14:34.920] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000838)  
So, viewed through this lens,

**[01:14:37.320] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000839)  
the proposed 2027 budget raises a number of serious concerns.

**[01:14:43.320] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000840)  
First, the apparent failure to prioritize the productive sectors,

**[01:14:49.320] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000841)  
particularly agriculture, trade, and industry,

**[01:14:53.600] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000842)  
which historically has been getting very low public investments,

**[01:14:58.160] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000843)  
and it is even lower this year.

**[01:15:00.000] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000844)  
These are critical to raising productivity, generating decent employment, and improving people's incomes.

**[01:15:07.740] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000845)  
The 2027 proposed budget raises debt service, public works, the defense sector, law and order, while significantly cutting the budget for agriculture, agrarian reform, and trade and industry.

**[01:15:22.340] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000846)  
These are the productivity-enhancing sectors, the productivity-enhancing agencies that promote people's incomes, people's livelihoods, and employments.

**[01:15:32.380] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000847)  
This tilts the budget towards fiscal obligations, infrastructure, defense, and law and order, and these are prioritized over enhancing productivity and increasing incomes for our people.

**[01:15:45.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000848)  
This must be addressed.

**[01:15:47.520] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000849)  
We are very, very disturbed by the lack of prioritization.

**[01:15:52.340] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000850)  
We are very, very disturbed by the lack of prioritization of productivity-enhancing economic activities which our people badly need, particularly in agriculture and MSMEs.

**[01:16:02.520] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000851)  
Second, so basically this imbalance raises a fundamental question.

**[01:16:07.020] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000852)  
How can we aspire to inclusive development without adequately investing in the productive capacities that enable Filipinos to secure decent work and lift themselves outside of poverty?

**[01:16:20.500] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000853)  
This is a structural transformation.

**[01:16:22.920] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000855)  
If we talk systemic, this is systemic.

**[01:16:26.880] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000856)  
Second, now we'll talk about human development.

**[01:16:32.060] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000857)  
The relegation of people's well-being, dignity, and human development has also been relegated to a secondary concern.

**[01:16:39.720] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000858)  
Infrastructure and debt service received the highest budgetary increases, while significant budget cuts have yet again been made to health, education, housing, and other essential social needs.

**[01:16:52.340] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000859)  
Now, this raises budgetary issues about the kinds of priorities of our policymakers.

**[01:17:00.460] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000860)  
I'm sorry to say, are we building the foundations for lasting human development, or are we merely managing the consequences of persistent poverty and inequality?

**[01:17:10.900] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000861)  
This is very residual social policy rather than transformative.

**[01:17:16.720] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000862)  
Third, and perhaps most troubling, is the persistence.

**[01:17:22.340] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000863)  
This is the result of appropriations vulnerable to pork barrel and patronage politics, which our colleagues in People's Budget Coalition have already very much focused on.

**[01:17:32.540] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000864)  
Our analysis identifies ₱221 billion of DPWH's BIP program in the House-approved budget, ₱72 billion above the proposed NEP allocation.

**[01:17:46.340] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000865)  
These practices are especially troubling in light of the Supreme Court's recent ruling.

**[01:17:52.040] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000866)  
This problem of political discretion, now we go to soft pork, extends to social protection programs, which are indispensable in a country marked by widespread poverty and inequality.

**[01:18:07.220] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000867)  
The House-approved GAB also contains what may be characterized as soft pork, and our colleagues in PBC have mentioned this in a very thorough way.

**[01:18:16.220] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000868)  
At the very least, we urge the Senate to restore their allocations.

**[01:18:22.040] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000869)  
to the NEP level, and the freed-up resources go to [unclear]. I will talk about that later in the recommendations.

**[01:18:28.340] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000870)  
But let us be clear, the problem is not social protection itself, but its appropriation for political ends.

**[01:18:37.020] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000871)  
So consider PhilHealth and MAIFIP, and my colleagues will talk about this much more extensively.

**[01:18:42.920] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000872)  
May pakalakang papel naman ang MAIFIP bilang safety net para sa mga pasyenteng kulang ang health coverage.

**[01:18:50.040] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000874)  
Ngunit meron na tayong PhilHealth eh.

**[01:18:53.040] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000875)  
Sa ilalim ng Universal Health Care Act, hindi dapat maging permanenteng kapalit ng PhilHealth sa isang parallel system ng medical assistance.

**[01:19:02.480] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000876)  
Kung kulang ang benepisyon ng PhilHealth sa tamang tugon, ito kasi ng PhilHealth.

**[01:19:08.000] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000877)  
Hindi patuloy napalakihin ang MAIFIP.

**[01:19:10.040] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000878)  
Yet in the proposed 2027 budget, PhilHealth remains inadequately funded to fulfill its universal mandate.

**[01:19:20.040] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000879)  
While MAIFIP retains substantial allocations, which we all know is vulnerable to patronage politics.

**[01:19:27.520] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000880)  
Napakasakit ang isang Pilipino, hindi niya dapat itanong, sino ang malalipitan ko?

**[01:19:33.500] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000881)  
Ang dapat niyang sasabihin, covered ako, karapatan ko to.

**[01:19:38.400] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000882)  
Now to LGSF, which stands at 62.62 billion from the HGAB, as we know, is also a kind of pork barrel,

**[01:19:46.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000883)  
especially the FALBU, the SBDP, and the DRR Assistance Program.

**[01:19:51.660] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000884)  
These are all discretionary funds, no?

**[01:19:54.060] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000885)  
And are highly vulnerable to patronage politics.

**[01:19:56.920] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000886)  
And let's face it, especially as 2028 nears.

**[01:20:01.100] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000887)  
Adding soft pork, so if we add soft pork that I talked about earlier with the hard pork of BIP and LGSF,

**[01:20:08.320] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000888)  
we at least identified about 427 billion in appropriations that warrant rigorous scrutiny

**[01:20:14.640] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000889)  
because of their extreme vulnerability.

**[01:20:16.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000890)  
to political patronage and corruption.

**[01:20:19.880] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000891)  
So this brings us to a fundamental question.

**[01:20:23.240] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000892)  
Whose interest does our national budget ultimately serve?

**[01:20:26.520] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000893)  
The Filipino people or those who wield political power?

**[01:20:30.180] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000894)  
We cannot ignore the still unresolved flood control corruption scandal involving hundreds of billions of pesos.

**[01:20:38.160] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000895)  
It remains an open wound in our national consciousness,

**[01:20:42.180] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000896)  
compounded by the continuing absence of meaningful accountability.

**[01:20:46.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000899)  
The Filipino people have not forgotten.

**[01:20:50.000] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000900)  
The recent Supreme Court rulings have reinforced long-standing civil society concerns

**[01:20:55.520] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000901)  
over unconstitutional budgetary practices, including unlawful congressional increases in appropriations,

**[01:21:03.620] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000902)  
weakened safeguards over universal appropriations,

**[01:21:07.400] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000903)  
prohibited lump-sum appropriations that enable discretionary project allocations,

**[01:21:12.400] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000904)  
including for flood control.

**[01:21:13.780] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000905)  
As our committee chair,

**[01:21:16.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000906)  
has said,

**[01:21:18.160] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000907)  
these revelations have profoundly eroded public trust,

**[01:21:21.960] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000908)  
fueling deep anger and a sense of betrayal.

**[01:21:26.380] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000909)  
As such, it is with a sense of urgency that we call on the Senate

**[01:21:29.360] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000910)  
to undertake five decisive actions.

**[01:21:32.640] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000911)  
First, scrutinize and remove the estimated 427 billion in appropriations vulnerable to hard and soft pork.

**[01:21:39.360] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000912)  
Mas malaki yung sa PBC.

**[01:21:41.740] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000913)  
Dapat isama talaga yan.

**[01:21:43.120] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000914)  
Second,

**[01:21:46.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000915)  
redirect these resources towards agriculture,

**[01:21:48.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000916)  
trade and industry

**[01:21:50.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000917)  
to raise productivity,

**[01:21:52.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000918)  
generate decent work,

**[01:21:54.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000919)  
and strengthen sustainable employment and livelihoods.

**[01:21:56.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000920)  
The most sustainable pathway out of poverty is decent work.

**[01:22:00.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000922)  
Let's put decent work up in the agenda.

**[01:22:04.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000924)  
Third,

**[01:22:06.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000925)  
increase investments in education,

**[01:22:08.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000926)  
health, housing,

**[01:22:10.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000927)  
social protection, child protection,

**[01:22:12.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000928)  
persons with disabilities,

**[01:22:14.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000929)  
and other socially excluded groups.

**[01:22:16.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000930)  
And seriously take into account

**[01:22:18.940] Social Watch Philippines Co-convenor Dr. Maria Victoria Raquiza** (asr-000931)  
Social Watch Philippines-ABI proposal,

**[01:29:28.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001146)  
And sixth, we need to

**[01:29:30.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001147)  
add more funding amidst the impending

**[01:29:32.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001148)  
Super El Nino. And of course,

**[01:29:34.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001149)  
lastly, Code NGO wishes

**[01:29:36.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001150)  
to expand and protect spaces for

**[01:29:38.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001151)  
CSOs to meaningfully contribute in

**[01:29:40.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001152)  
the budget. So currently in the

**[01:29:42.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001153)  
House, there are bills

**[01:29:44.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001154)  
pending on civil society participation

**[01:29:46.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001155)  
in the budgeting process and we wish

**[01:29:48.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001156)

**[01:29:50.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001157)

**[01:29:52.940] CODE-NGO Representative Catherine Basalote** (asr-001158)

**[01:29:54.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001159)
