**[01:29:56.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001160)  
Next, from Citizens Watch

**[01:29:58.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001161)  
Philippines.

**[01:30:01.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001162)  
As represented by co-convenor, Ms. Julia Christine Baza.

**[01:30:04.880] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001163)  
Good afternoon, Honorable Chair Ejército, Senate President Katchilyan, and Vice Chair Senator Lawson.

**[01:30:12.740] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001164)  
We thank the Senate Committee on Finance for inviting civil society organizations to consult regarding the proposed 2027 budget.

**[01:30:21.080] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001165)  
As Congress continues to deliberate, Citizen Watch calls for greater consumer-centered public spending that prioritizes affordability, accessibility, and effective delivery of essential services.

**[01:30:35.100] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001166)  
We recognize the importance of adequate government funding for better public service.

**[01:30:40.440] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001167)  
However, we also emphasize that increased budget allocations must translate into tangible, measurable, and meaningful benefits for Filipino consumers.

**[01:30:51.080] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001168)  
Public spending must prioritize affordable and reliable electricity, accessible healthcare, safe and efficient public transportation, effective flood protection, improved digital services, and stronger consumer protection from the raging cost of living crisis.

**[01:31:11.820] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001169)  
We call on Congress and concerned government agencies to ensure that the proposed 2027 budget is implemented.

**[01:31:21.080] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001170)  
With transparency, accountability, efficient resource utilization, and strict monitoring of government projects and programs.

**[01:31:29.980] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001171)  
Investments must address the everyday needs of Filipino households, particularly low-income and vulnerable communities, while ensuring that public resources are not wasted through inefficiencies, delays, or questionable expenditures.

**[01:31:46.200] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001172)  
On healthcare, we welcome the proposed increase in the Cancer Assistance Fund.

**[01:31:54.140] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001174)  
And if approved, we urge the Department of Health to ensure that these additional resources are utilized efficiently and without delay to reach more patients and improve access to comprehensive cancer care.

**[01:32:06.680] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001175)  
Citizen Watch Philippines previously worked with Senator J.V. Ejercito as the principal author of the National Integrated Cancer Control Act.

**[01:32:15.080] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001176)  
At the same time, we emphasize the need to adequately fund our public education system.

**[01:32:21.080] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001177)  
As Education Secretary Angara has pointed out, funding gaps in the proposed 2027 budget could slow implementation of critical programs.

**[01:32:31.220] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001178)  
We therefore support efforts to restore and augment the funding for these essential programs.

**[01:32:37.660] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001179)  
Every peso of taxpayers' money must serve the public interest.

**[01:32:42.160] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001180)  
The true measure of the 2027 national budget should be whether Filipino families experiencing lower household expenses,

**[01:32:51.080] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001181)  
better access to essential services, greater protection from financial burdens, and meaningful improvements in their quality of life.

**[01:33:00.420] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001182)  
For Citizen Watch Philippines, a truly responsive national budget is one whose benefits are not only reflected in government reports and spending figures,

**[01:33:10.560] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001183)  
but are genuinely felt by ordinary Filipinos in their homes and in their communities.

**[01:33:15.540] CitizenWatch Philippines Co-convenor Julia Kristine Basa** (asr-001184)  
Thank you.

**[01:33:15.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001185)  
Thank you.

**[01:33:21.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001186)  
I'm very happy to hear about your observations.

**[01:33:23.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001187)  
From infrastructure to healthcare and cancer, you mentioned about cancer care.

**[01:33:31.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001188)  
Actually, when we push the National Integrated Cancer Control Act,

**[01:33:33.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001189)  
we're hoping that more hospitals, every year, accredited hospitals that can avail of the National Integrated Cancer Control Act,

**[01:33:51.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001190)

**[01:33:51.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001191)

**[01:33:52.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001192)  
But we're still in the proof.

**[01:33:54.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001193)  
We'll see to it that it will be added.

**[01:33:56.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001194)

**[01:34:03.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001195)

**[01:34:05.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001196)

**[01:34:13.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001198)

**[01:34:16.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001199)  
I also said that I would like to increase the subsidy of PhilHealth so that their participation,

**[01:34:21.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001200)  
ng field health

**[01:34:23.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001201)  
yung kanilang participation

**[01:34:24.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001202)  
will be felt.

**[01:34:27.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001203)  
At least 30, 40, 50 percent

**[01:34:30.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001204)  
or ng bill

**[01:34:32.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001205)  
that will at least be a big help

**[01:34:34.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001206)  
to lower the out-of-pocket expense

**[01:34:36.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001207)  
of every Filipino.

**[01:34:37.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001208)  
That's our real goal.

**[01:34:42.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001209)  
So thank you for those.

**[01:34:44.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001210)  
But we take note of those.

**[01:36:20.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001259)  
FOI Youth Initiative or FYI.

**[01:36:24.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001260)  
Mr. Aljon Patrick Acupan.

**[01:36:27.140] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001261)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair,

**[01:36:28.640] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001262)  
and good afternoon to everyone.

**[01:36:30.520] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001263)  
First, we want to thank the Senate

**[01:36:32.500] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001264)  
for recognizing the role of the CSOs

**[01:36:35.040] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001265)  
and for acknowledging the role

**[01:36:36.380] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001266)  
that we play in nation building.

**[01:36:38.320] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001267)  
And I want to start by saying

**[01:36:39.820] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001268)  
that the cost of learning

**[01:36:41.000] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001269)  
for civil society government,

**[01:36:42.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001270)  
civil society organizations,

**[01:36:43.820] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001271)  
and the government alike

**[01:36:44.780] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001272)  
when it comes to protecting the budget

**[01:36:46.800] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001273)  
is already so high.

**[01:36:48.660] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001274)  
And we are at this point, actually,

**[01:36:50.240] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001275)  
that we are not able to do so.

**[01:36:50.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001276)  
We are now, should be talking about

**[01:36:52.080] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001277)  
how we could prevent that

**[01:36:53.160] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001278)  
from happening further.

**[01:36:54.620] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001279)  
So my organization is really about

**[01:36:56.240] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001280)  
the campaigning for the right to information.

**[01:36:58.680] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001281)  
And we already thank the Senate

**[01:36:59.760] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001282)  
for doing its job in passing

**[01:37:02.260] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001283)  
the right to information bill

**[01:37:03.680] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001284)  
that has been waiting for action

**[01:37:05.160] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001285)  
for almost three decades now.

**[01:37:07.300] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001286)  
And we are looking forward

**[01:37:08.740] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001287)  
to the time that

**[01:37:09.680] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001288)  
the Bicameral Conference Committee

**[01:37:12.260] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001289)  
would convene to finally pass it to a law.

**[01:37:14.900] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001290)  
Now we are here to support

**[01:37:16.520] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001291)  
the demand for transparency

**[01:37:18.420] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001292)  
in the budget process.

**[01:37:19.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001293)  
The Chair has recognized

**[01:37:20.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001294)  
and rightly raised the need

**[01:37:22.140] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001295)  
to restore public trust in government

**[01:37:24.080] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001296)  
and in the budget process.

**[01:37:25.880] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001297)  
I believe we must recognize

**[01:37:27.520] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001298)  
the trust is not restored

**[01:37:28.880] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001299)  
simply by hoping that

**[01:37:30.520] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001300)  
the public should trust our institutions.

**[01:37:33.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001301)  
It is restored

**[01:37:33.980] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001302)  
when citizens feel that

**[01:37:35.940] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001303)  
our reforms deliver.

**[01:37:37.900] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001304)  
We can start by making citizens

**[01:37:39.980] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001305)  
independently examine government decisions,

**[01:37:43.100] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001306)  
understand how public money

**[01:37:44.740] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001307)  
is allocated and spent,

**[01:37:46.700] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001308)  
and see whether these decisions

**[01:37:48.540] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001309)  
respond to their needs.

**[01:37:50.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001310)  
This is where access to information

**[01:37:52.620] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001311)  
becomes indispensable.

**[01:37:54.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001312)  
Specifically on the budget process,

**[01:37:56.680] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001313)  
it matters not only when information

**[01:37:58.940] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001314)  
is not disclosed.

**[01:38:00.220] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001315)  
As an advocate,

**[01:38:01.100] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001316)  
it matters when transparency is absent

**[01:38:04.500] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001317)  
or when we lack transparency,

**[01:38:07.320] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001318)  
or when information is not released

**[01:38:09.480] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001319)  
or on time,

**[01:38:10.560] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001320)  
or if it is not presented in a way

**[01:38:12.580] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001321)  
that our citizens could understand.

**[01:38:15.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001322)  
This makes participation difficult

**[01:38:17.260] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001323)  
for citizens and weakens public scrutiny.

**[01:38:20.060] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001324)  
Worse,

**[01:38:20.820] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001325)  
even when people are invited

**[01:38:22.320] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001326)  
in consultations,

**[01:38:23.640] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001327)  
they may have little to no basis

**[01:38:26.100] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001328)  
to assess whether these contributions

**[01:38:28.120] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001329)  
actually matter.

**[01:38:29.580] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001330)  
With existing efforts in place,

**[01:38:31.540] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001331)  
we encourage the Senate

**[01:38:32.960] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001332)  
to lead further reforms

**[01:38:34.620] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001333)  
that are rooted in increasing transparency,

**[01:38:37.640] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001334)  
traceability, and citizen oversight

**[01:38:39.340] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001335)  
to prevent backroom attempts

**[01:38:41.080] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001336)  
to game the budget process.

**[01:38:43.240] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001337)  
And I will now

**[01:38:45.000] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001338)  
critique on some of the

**[01:38:46.820] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001339)  
transparency initiatives

**[01:38:48.520] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001340)  
that I believe should be strengthened.

**[01:38:50.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001341)  
First, on live streaming the budget hearings.

**[01:38:54.040] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001342)  
We acknowledge that this is a very important step

**[01:38:56.440] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001343)  
that the people could follow

**[01:38:58.200] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001344)  
on their mobile phones

**[01:39:01.360] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001345)  
what is happening on the floor.

**[01:39:03.280] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001346)  
But without access to records

**[01:39:05.100] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001347)  
and what are the assumptions

**[01:39:06.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001348)  
that came with this budget proposal,

**[01:39:08.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001349)  
it makes participation

**[01:39:09.860] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001350)  
a piecemeal attempt for the CSOs.

**[01:39:13.860] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001351)  
And what is the value

**[01:39:14.940] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001352)  
of live streaming deliberations

**[01:39:16.380] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001353)  
if the substantive negotiations

**[01:39:18.160] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001354)  
have already taken place

**[01:39:19.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001355)  
in closed-term meetings?

**[01:39:20.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001356)  
And what does transparency mean

**[01:39:22.500] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001357)  
if citizens can watch

**[01:39:23.800] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001358)  
the proceedings but cannot access

**[01:39:26.020] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001359)  
the underlying documents

**[01:39:27.700] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001360)  
and proposed amendments

**[01:39:28.760] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001361)  
and compare the different versions at ease?

**[01:39:31.880] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001362)  
Second, on abolishing

**[01:39:33.200] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001363)  
closed-door small committee.

**[01:39:35.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001364)  
Does abolishing a particular committee

**[01:39:37.240] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001365)  
eliminate opacity

**[01:39:38.260] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001366)  
if similar negotiations

**[01:39:39.700] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001367)  
simply migrate to other informal

**[01:39:41.840] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001368)  
or undocumented settings?

**[01:39:44.280] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001369)  
This is the kind of assurance

**[01:39:45.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001370)  
that our people deserve,

**[01:39:47.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001371)  
that everything is done transparently

**[01:39:50.720] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001372)  
over all these reforms

**[01:39:53.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001373)  
may be important steps,

**[01:39:55.100] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001374)  
but we must not confuse

**[01:39:56.360] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001375)  
the existence of transparency mechanism

**[01:39:58.580] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001376)  
with the achievement of transparency.

**[01:40:01.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001377)  
Information must be available

**[01:40:03.100] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001378)  
before, during, and after decisions.

**[01:40:06.760] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001379)  
This also follows the budget process.

**[01:40:09.420] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001380)  
Records must allow the public

**[01:40:11.040] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001381)  
to reconstruct and scrutinize

**[01:40:12.800] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001382)  
those documents.

**[01:40:14.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001383)  
And disclosures must establish

**[01:40:16.780] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001384)  
not only what was spent,

**[01:40:18.760] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001385)  
but who decided

**[01:40:19.760] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001386)  
and on what basis.

**[01:40:20.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001387)  
And with what results.

**[01:40:23.400] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001388)  
We turn our attention

**[01:40:24.300] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001389)  
to how an effective

**[01:40:25.640] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001390)  
and inclusive budget process

**[01:40:27.140] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001391)  
could deliver felt outcomes

**[01:40:28.740] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001392)  
for the people.

**[01:40:30.120] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001393)  
Make the budget outcome-based.

**[01:40:32.440] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001394)  
We must be able to determine

**[01:40:33.840] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001395)  
whether public spending

**[01:40:35.080] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001396)  
improved learning outcomes,

**[01:40:37.200] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001397)  
expanded access to healthcare,

**[01:40:39.180] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001398)  
reduced poverty,

**[01:40:40.460] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001399)  
improved agricultural productivity,

**[01:40:42.180] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001400)  
or delivered

**[01:40:42.800] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001401)  
where the results

**[01:40:44.000] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001402)  
the program promised.

**[01:40:46.240] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001403)  
This requires accessible

**[01:40:47.620] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001404)  
performance indicators,

**[01:40:50.220] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001405)  
baseline,

**[01:40:50.600] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001406)  
targets,

**[01:40:51.320] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001407)  
and actual results

**[01:40:52.120] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001408)  
that citizens can compare

**[01:40:53.360] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001409)  
against expenditures.

**[01:40:55.740] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001410)  
In closing,

**[01:40:56.660] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001411)  
thank you for your time.

**[01:40:58.260] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001412)  
We just want to say

**[01:40:58.980] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001413)  
that claiming transparency

**[01:40:59.960] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001414)  
in the face of today's issue

**[01:41:01.620] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001415)  
give transparency

**[01:41:03.040] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001416)  
a very bad name.

**[01:41:04.140] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001417)  
And we deserve

**[01:41:04.840] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001418)  
real and genuine transparency.

**[01:41:07.080] FOI Youth Initiative Co-convenor Aljon Patrick Acupan** (asr-001419)  
Thank you.

**[01:41:08.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001420)  
Okay, thank you.

**[01:41:10.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001421)  
Next is Action for Economic Reforms.

**[01:41:14.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001422)  
Attorney Paula May.

**[01:41:17.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001423)  
Pang Kien.

**[01:41:18.640] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001424)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair.

**[01:41:19.800] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001425)  
Good afternoon,

**[01:41:20.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001426)  
Sen. President Gatchalian

**[01:41:23.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001427)  
and Sen. Lacson

**[01:41:23.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001428)  
and our friends from the Civil Society.

**[01:41:25.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001429)  
So we thank you for giving us time

**[01:41:27.120] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001430)  
to share our comments

**[01:41:28.180] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001431)  
to scrutinize the 2027 budget.

**[01:41:31.420] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001432)  
So, Mr. Chair,

**[01:41:32.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001433)  
nung pinaglaban po natin

**[01:41:33.240] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001434)  
ang sin tax reform

**[01:41:34.100] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001435)  
at ang universal healthcare law

**[01:41:35.520] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001436)  
na kayo ang champion,

**[01:41:37.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001437)  
we all share the same vision

**[01:41:38.800] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001438)  
that no Filipino will die

**[01:41:40.280] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001439)  
because they could not access healthcare

**[01:41:42.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001440)  
where health is a right

**[01:41:43.720] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001441)  
and not a privilege.

**[01:41:45.180] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001442)  
But these promises, Mr. Chair,

**[01:41:47.180] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001443)  
need not only political will

**[01:41:48.520] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001444)  
to be fully implemented

**[01:41:49.580] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001445)  
but also,

**[01:41:50.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001446)  
adequate funding.

**[01:41:51.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001447)  
That is why

**[01:41:52.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001448)  
the law also provided

**[01:41:53.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001449)  
for sources of funds

**[01:41:54.680] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001450)  
to ensure it meets its objectives.

**[01:41:57.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001451)  
But since a few years back,

**[01:41:58.460] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001452)  
we have witnessed

**[01:41:59.040] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001453)  
the systematic undermining

**[01:42:00.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001454)  
of that very promise

**[01:42:02.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001455)  
through the national budget.

**[01:42:03.620] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001456)  
And that is the glaring underfunding

**[01:42:05.280] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001457)  
of the PhilHealth.

**[01:42:06.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001458)  
For 2027,

**[01:42:08.320] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001459)  
the proposed PhilHealth budget

**[01:42:10.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001460)  
stands at only 74.45 billion

**[01:42:13.940] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001461)  
against a projected funding requirement

**[01:42:16.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001462)  
of approximately 244 billion minimum,

**[01:42:19.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001463)  
which means

**[01:42:20.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001464)  
one where PhilHealth said

**[01:42:22.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001465)  
would mean status quo

**[01:42:24.200] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001466)  
or no new benefits

**[01:42:25.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001467)  
compared to what

**[01:42:26.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001468)  
they actuarially estimated

**[01:42:28.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001469)  
with expansion of benefits

**[01:42:30.200] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001470)  
to be at 376 billion.

**[01:42:32.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001471)  
So at the minimum,

**[01:42:34.040] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001472)  
that leaves us

**[01:42:34.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001473)  
an estimated funding gap

**[01:42:36.340] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001474)  
of around 170 billion.

**[01:42:39.480] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001475)  
Based on the 74.45 budget,

**[01:42:42.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001476)  
55 billion is premiums

**[01:42:45.180] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001477)  
for the indirect contributors,

**[01:42:47.140] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001478)  
whereas the remaining

**[01:42:47.980] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001479)  
is for benefits enhancement.

**[01:42:49.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001480)  
That means that

**[01:42:50.980] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001481)  
sin taxes which are used

**[01:42:52.860] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001482)  
as premium payments

**[01:42:53.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001483)  
for the indirect contributors

**[01:42:55.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001484)  
is short of around 20 billion

**[01:42:57.480] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001485)  
of what the government

**[01:42:59.180] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001486)  
actually collected

**[01:43:00.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001487)  
from sin taxes

**[01:43:02.000] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001488)  
amounting to 70 billion.

**[01:43:04.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001489)  
Despite the DBM admission

**[01:43:06.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001490)  
that this is not

**[01:43:08.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001491)  
sin tax compliant,

**[01:43:10.060] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001492)  
the lower house

**[01:43:12.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001493)  
still did not restore

**[01:43:12.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001494)  
the legally mandated sin tax share.

**[01:43:15.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001495)  
Mr. Chair,

**[01:43:15.980] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001496)  
this is not only

**[01:43:16.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001497)  
a question of inadequate funding.

**[01:43:19.180] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001498)  
This is,

**[01:43:19.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001499)  
a question of compliance

**[01:43:21.100] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001500)  
with the law.

**[01:43:22.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001501)  
In Pimentel v. House of Representatives,

**[01:43:25.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001502)  
which we fought hard for

**[01:43:27.000] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001503)  
together with many of the CSOs

**[01:43:29.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001504)  
here present,

**[01:43:30.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001505)  
the Supreme Court

**[01:43:31.000] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001506)  
in a preparatory statement

**[01:43:32.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001507)  
said clearly

**[01:43:33.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001508)  
that the right to health

**[01:43:34.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001509)  
is not merely important.

**[01:43:36.420] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001510)  
It is enabling,

**[01:43:37.340] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001511)  
elevating, empowering,

**[01:43:38.860] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001512)  
and a government

**[01:43:39.500] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001513)  
that protects this right

**[01:43:40.560] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001514)  
affirms the value

**[01:43:41.420] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001515)  
of every human life,

**[01:43:42.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001516)  
but the right needs resources.

**[01:43:45.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001517)  
Health is persistently underfunded,

**[01:43:47.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001518)  
a paradox given

**[01:43:48.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001519)  
how foundational

**[01:43:49.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001520)  
it is.

**[01:43:50.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001521)  
Why does the most immediate right

**[01:43:51.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001522)  
remain the most neglected?

**[01:43:53.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001523)  
In the same case,

**[01:43:54.940] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001524)  
the Supreme Court

**[01:43:55.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001525)  
also affirmed that

**[01:43:56.500] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001526)  
sin taxes legally earmarked

**[01:43:58.240] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001527)  
for universal health care

**[01:43:59.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001528)  
are special funds

**[01:44:00.380] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001529)  
and must be allocated

**[01:44:01.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001530)  
in accordance with the law.

**[01:44:03.320] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001531)  
To quote the SC decision,

**[01:44:04.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001532)  
they said,

**[01:44:05.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001533)  
the funds of PhilHealth

**[01:44:06.040] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001534)  
are special funds

**[01:44:06.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001535)  
created for a special purpose

**[01:44:08.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001536)  
contemplated under

**[01:44:10.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001537)  
the Constitution.

**[01:44:11.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001538)  
While its funds

**[01:44:12.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001539)  
may be unspent

**[01:44:13.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001540)  
at a given time,

**[01:44:14.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001541)  
there will still be

**[01:44:15.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001542)  
no unobligated funds

**[01:44:16.580] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001543)  
to speak of

**[01:44:17.380] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001544)  
as they are exclusively

**[01:44:18.680] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001545)  
appropriated

**[01:44:19.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001546)  
for the people's

**[01:44:20.220] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001547)  
right to health.

**[01:44:21.040] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001548)  
In short,

**[01:44:21.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001549)  
these earmarked funds

**[01:44:22.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001550)  
are legally reserved

**[01:44:23.520] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001551)  
for health care.

**[01:44:24.680] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001552)  
Congress cannot simply

**[01:44:25.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001553)  
disregard the statutory obligations

**[01:44:27.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001554)  
through the annual

**[01:44:28.380] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001555)  
appropriation process.

**[01:44:30.040] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001556)  
Compliance is therefore

**[01:44:31.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001557)  
not optional,

**[01:44:32.320] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001558)  
Mr. Chair.

**[01:44:33.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001559)  
And what makes this

**[01:44:33.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001560)  
even more problematic

**[01:44:34.860] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001561)  
is that while PhilHealth

**[01:44:36.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001562)  
faces massive funding gap,

**[01:44:38.280] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001563)  
the lower house

**[01:44:39.240] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001564)  
continues to expand

**[01:44:40.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001565)  
discretionary

**[01:44:42.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001566)  
medical assistance program

**[01:44:43.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001567)  
when we are told

**[01:44:44.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001568)  
that there is not enough

**[01:44:45.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001569)  
money for PhilHealth.

**[01:44:47.560] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001570)  
Nasabi na rin po

**[01:44:48.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001571)  
ng marami natin

**[01:44:49.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001572)  
kasamahan dito

**[01:44:50.240] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001573)  
na dinoble

**[01:44:50.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001574)  
ng lower house

**[01:44:51.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001575)  
ang maifip.

**[01:44:53.500] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001576)  
While through

**[01:44:54.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001577)  
the leadership

**[01:44:55.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001578)  
of Sen. Win

**[01:44:56.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001579)  
as the chairman

**[01:44:56.860] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001580)  
of the Committee

**[01:44:57.980] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001581)  
on Finance

**[01:44:58.560] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001582)  
last year,

**[01:44:59.460] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001583)  
natanggal

**[01:45:00.000] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001599)  
naman po talaga ang GLs

**[01:45:02.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001600)  
in the 2026

**[01:45:03.720] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001601)  
budget special provision.

**[01:45:05.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001602)  
Kaya lang po, in practice, political

**[01:45:07.800] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001603)  
patronage pa rin po. Hindi lang lumalabas

**[01:45:09.940] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001604)  
ang pangalan ng politiko sa bills

**[01:45:12.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001605)  
na binabayaran natin sa mga

**[01:45:13.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001606)  
hospitals. But in practice, ganun

**[01:45:15.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001607)  
pa rin po talaga siya. So,

**[01:45:17.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001608)  
alam naman po natin na when PhilHealth is

**[01:45:19.760] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001609)  
inadequate, is inadequately funded,

**[01:45:22.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001610)  
benefit expansion is constrained.

**[01:45:24.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001611)  
So, patients continue to pay out of pocket,

**[01:45:26.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001612)  
families borrow money, sell their possessions,

**[01:45:28.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001613)  
or delay treatment because

**[01:45:29.760] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001614)  
they cannot afford it. These are

**[01:45:31.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001615)  
just numbers in budget documents.

**[01:45:33.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001616)  
These are patients who cannot afford treatment

**[01:45:35.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001617)  
or medication. These are patients

**[01:45:37.620] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001618)  
who even forego seeking medical help.

**[01:45:40.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001619)  
These are Filipinos whom the

**[01:45:41.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001620)  
Universal Health Care Act was enacted to protect.

**[01:45:44.400] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001621)  
And every time we fail to provide

**[01:45:45.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001622)  
the funding necessary to implement

**[01:45:47.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001623)  
that law, we fail these patients.

**[01:45:50.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001624)  
That is why we are calling on the

**[01:45:51.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001625)  
Senate to respectfully do

**[01:45:53.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001626)  
the following. So, first,

**[01:45:55.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001627)  
we call on the Senate to fully account for and

**[01:45:57.800] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001628)  
allocate all legally

**[01:45:59.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001629)  
earmarked funds for universal health care.

**[01:46:02.320] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001630)  
From years back, the

**[01:46:03.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001631)  
unappropriated funds due to PhilHealth

**[01:46:05.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001632)  
from sin taxes and

**[01:46:06.900] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001633)  
PagCorp PCS is still around

**[01:46:09.400] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001634)  
200 billion po. While yung

**[01:46:11.400] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001635)  
appropriated but unallocated

**[01:46:13.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001636)  
amounts to 112 billion.

**[01:46:15.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001637)  
So, that is around 300 plus billion

**[01:46:17.580] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001638)  
po as of this

**[01:46:19.220] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001639)  
time. These funds are not—

**[01:46:21.500] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001640)  
they are public funds dedicated

**[01:46:23.500] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001641)  
by law to protecting the health of the Filipinos.

**[01:46:25.860] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001642)  
They should be given to PhilHealth.

**[01:46:27.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001643)  
And second, we still have

**[01:46:29.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001644)  
to address the 170 billion

**[01:46:31.120] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001645)  
PhilHealth funding gap. The government

**[01:46:33.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001646)  
must present a credible financing

**[01:46:35.400] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001647)  
plan that will allow PhilHealth to meet

**[01:46:37.220] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001648)  
its obligations, expand benefits

**[01:46:39.480] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001649)  
and reduce out-of-pocket spending.

**[01:46:41.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001650)  
Even with the unappropriated

**[01:46:43.480] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001651)  
and appropriated but unallocated

**[01:46:45.780] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001652)  
funds, which we still urge

**[01:46:47.520] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001653)  
Congress to give to PhilHealth,

**[01:46:49.480] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001654)  
this budget is still necessary to

**[01:46:51.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001655)  
comply with statutory requirements

**[01:46:53.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001656)  
and ensure the financial

**[01:46:55.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001657)  
sustainability of PhilHealth.

**[01:46:57.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001658)  
We reiterate that under

**[01:46:59.360] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001659)  
the UHC Act, the national government

**[01:47:01.540] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001660)  
is responsible for subsidizing

**[01:47:03.620] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001661)  
the premiums of indirect contributors.

**[01:47:06.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001662)  
In addition, various laws

**[01:47:07.640] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001663)  
earmark specific revenues,

**[01:47:09.820] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001664)  
including portions of sin tax collection

**[01:47:11.660] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001665)  
for healthcare financing.

**[01:47:13.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001666)  
These funding obligations should not

**[01:47:15.700] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001667)  
be treated as optional

**[01:47:17.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001668)  
simply because PhilHealth has accumulated

**[01:47:19.800] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001669)  
funds. The government

**[01:47:21.440] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001670)  
must comply with the applicable

**[01:47:23.460] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001671)  
statutory requirements governing

**[01:47:25.300] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001672)  
appropriations and financing.

**[01:47:27.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001673)  
In this regard, ang importante

**[01:47:29.200] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001674)  
nating tanong is hindi kakayanin

**[01:47:31.460] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001675)  
ba ni PhilHealth ang funding gap,

**[01:47:33.320] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001676)  
temporary funding gap, kakayanin bang

**[01:47:35.100] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001677)  
i-absorb ni PhilHealth ang temporary funding gap?

**[01:47:37.280] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001678)  
Kung hindi, nagko-comply ba

**[01:47:39.460] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001679)  
ang gobyerno sa legal obligations

**[01:47:41.460] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001680)  
and maintaining a financial

**[01:47:43.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001681)  
sustainable UHC system?

**[01:47:45.840] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001682)  
Finally, we call

**[01:47:46.960] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001683)  
that Congress demonstrate

**[01:47:49.220] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001684)  
full compliance with the Supreme Court's

**[01:47:51.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001685)  
decision. Congress and the executive

**[01:47:52.980] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001686)  
must explain how the national budget

**[01:47:55.140] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001687)  
accounts for the legally mandated health

**[01:47:57.280] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001688)  
allocation. Mr. Chair,

**[01:47:59.200] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001689)  
ang universal healthcare po is

**[01:48:01.160] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001690)  
a hard-worn reform.

**[01:48:03.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001691)  
So, government cannot just

**[01:48:05.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001692)  
sell passage of this law while refusing

**[01:48:07.240] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001693)  
to honor the funding commitments that

**[01:48:09.140] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001694)  
make it possible. We demand

**[01:48:10.940] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001695)  
compliance with the law and accountability

**[01:48:12.740] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001696)  
for the promises made to the Filipino people

**[01:48:15.220] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001697)  
because as we have repeatedly said,

**[01:48:17.260] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001698)  
Mr. Chair, health is a right and not

**[01:48:19.140] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-001699)  
a privilege. Thank you po.

**[01:48:21.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001700)  
Thank you, Atty. Paula

**[01:48:23.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001701)  
Mae Tanquieng. Anyway,

**[01:48:25.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001702)  
we share your

**[01:48:26.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001703)  
your advocacies.

**[01:48:29.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001704)  
Magkasama tayo ng UHC

**[01:48:31.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001705)  
and frustrations as well

**[01:48:33.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001706)  
and hopefully this time

**[01:48:35.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001707)  
around that I'm chair already, sabi nga nila

**[01:48:37.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001708)  
yung perks of being

**[01:48:38.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001709)  
financier, at least

**[01:48:40.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001710)  
the programs that are

**[01:48:42.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001711)  
closest to your heart can now be

**[01:48:44.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001712)  
given priority and importance.

**[01:48:47.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001713)  
But let's assure that we will do our best

**[01:48:48.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001714)  
that we intend to

**[01:48:51.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001715)  
increase, as I

**[01:48:53.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001716)  
mentioned earlier, the subsidy for PhilHealth

**[01:48:55.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001717)  
para mas ma-increase yung benefits,

**[01:48:58.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001718)  
mag-improve yung packages.

**[01:48:59.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001719)  
And yung participation.

**[01:49:00.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001720)  
Ang target natin is to lower

**[01:49:02.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001721)  
the out-of-pocket expense of every Filipino.

however,
