**[02:13:52.120] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002602)  
Philippine Academy

**[02:13:53.200] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002603)  
of Occupational Therapies,

**[02:13:54.600] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002604)  
Philippine Pharmacists Association.

**[02:13:56.260] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002605)  
At yung statement po

**[02:13:57.180] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002606)  
na babasahin ko po

**[02:13:58.080] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002607)  
ay pinilimahan po

**[02:13:59.620] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002608)  
ng aming mga miyembro.

**[02:14:02.200] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002609)  
So,

**[02:14:02.700] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002610)  
the members of HPAC

**[02:14:04.820] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002611)  
respectfully express

**[02:14:06.960] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002612)  
our deep concern

**[02:14:07.860] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002613)  
over the proposed

**[02:14:08.740] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002614)  
2027

**[02:14:10.920] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002615)  
General Appropriations Bill.

**[02:14:12.920] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002616)  
We strongly oppose

**[02:14:14.020] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002617)  
two specific provisions,

**[02:14:18.440] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002618)  
the proposed

**[02:14:22.120] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002619)  
74 billion PhilHealth allocation

**[02:14:23.420] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002620)  
for 2027

**[02:14:24.440] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002621)  
and the increase

**[02:14:26.040] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002622)  
in the budget of my FIP.

**[02:14:28.780] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002623)  
On the 74 billion PhilHealth allocation

**[02:14:31.120] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002624)  
for 2027,

**[02:14:32.300] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002625)  
we believe this is grossly inadequate

**[02:14:33.880] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002626)  
and will rapidly be exhausted.

**[02:14:36.920] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002627)  
This amount is projected

**[02:14:37.900] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002628)  
to sustain PhilHealth

**[02:14:38.800] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002629)  
only until approximately

**[02:14:40.860] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002630)  
March 2027

**[02:14:42.500] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002631)  
and after that,

**[02:14:43.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002632)  
the burden of healthcare costs

**[02:14:44.720] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002633)  
will increasingly fall

**[02:14:45.700] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002634)  
on patients themselves,

**[02:14:47.560] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002635)  
including direct contributors

**[02:14:48.820] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002636)  
who already pay into the system

**[02:14:50.800] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002637)  
but are not receiving

**[02:14:51.920] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002638)  
benefits commensurate

**[02:14:52.900] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002639)  
with their contributions.

**[02:14:54.780] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002640)  
This is not an isolated

**[02:14:56.180] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002641)  
budgetary shortfall.

**[02:14:58.200] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002642)  
The chronic underfunding of

**[02:14:59.520] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002643)  
PhilHealth represents a continuing failure

**[02:15:01.400] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002650)  
to fulfill the government's obligations under the Universal Health Care Act.

**[02:15:06.800] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002651)  
Since 2022, approximately P311 billion in earmarked revenues from Syntaxes, PCSO, and Pagcor

**[02:15:17.160] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002652)  
have been withheld or left unappropriated.

**[02:15:21.040] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002653)  
These resources were intended to help finance the UHC reforms

**[02:15:27.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002654)  
and should not be diverted, withheld, or left idle

**[02:15:31.880] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002655)  
when Filipinos continue to face catastrophic out-of-pocket health care costs.

**[02:15:37.720] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002656)  
The second provision that we have deep concern is the increase in the allocation for the MAIFIP

**[02:15:46.700] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002657)  
or the Medical Assistance for Indigent and Financially Incapacitated Patients

**[02:15:50.380] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002658)  
from approximately P24 billion to nearly P50 billion.

**[02:15:55.420] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002659)  
We understand that while financial assistance

**[02:15:57.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002660)  
to patients in need is essential,

**[02:16:00.580] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002661)  
the current system is fundamentally inequitable.

**[02:16:05.220] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002662)  
We observe, our members see that tens of thousands of Filipinos

**[02:16:10.560] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002663)  
are arbitrarily denied of MAIFIP benefits.

**[02:16:14.240] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002664)  
They come to us asking for help, yet we are often powerless

**[02:16:18.260] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002665)  
because of access to these funds depends on the discretion of our local officials.

**[02:16:27.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002666)  
Health care should not depend on whom a patient knows,

**[02:16:30.600] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002667)  
whom they can approach, or whom they can persuade to help them.

**[02:16:34.340] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002668)  
The amount of assistance a patient receives should be determined

**[02:16:37.580] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002669)  
by the nature and severity of their illness and the health care they require,

**[02:16:41.300] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002670)  
not by political connections.

**[02:16:43.140] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002671)  
MAIFIP has consequently become vulnerable to use

**[02:16:46.080] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002672)  
as a political instrument and a mechanism of patronage.

**[02:16:50.160] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002673)  
Like what other resource persons mentioned here,

**[02:16:53.420] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002674)  
we advocate for the abolition of the discretionary,

**[02:16:57.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002675)  
medical assistance funds such as MAIFIP

**[02:16:59.460] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002676)  
and the transfer of these resources to PhilHealth,

**[02:17:03.160] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002677)  
where they can be administered through transparent,

**[02:17:05.720] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002678)  
equitable, and rules-based mechanisms

**[02:17:07.760] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002679)  
that ensures assistance is determined by health care needs

**[02:17:12.300] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002680)  
and ability to pay, not political connections.

**[02:17:16.200] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002681)  
So ito po yung plea ng mga miyembro po ng HPAC.

**[02:17:20.300] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002682)  
Ito po yung apat na plea namin.

**[02:17:21.440] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002683)  
Una, immediate correction of the 2027 PhilHealth funding gap.

**[02:17:25.800] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002684)  
We urge the Congress and the...

**[02:17:27.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002685)  
during the budget amendment process

**[02:17:29.600] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002686)  
to decisively close the approximately 170 billion funding shortfall

**[02:17:34.340] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002687)  
and provide PhilHealth with at least the full 244 billion

**[02:17:39.460] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002688)  
minimum subsidy requirement for 2027.

**[02:17:42.940] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002689)  
Second, full remittance and appropriation of PhilHealth funds.

**[02:17:47.200] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002690)  
We call for the immediate release and appropriation

**[02:17:49.760] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002691)  
of the cumulative 311 billion in PhilHealth,

**[02:17:53.340] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002692)  
unappropriated and unreleased syntax, pag-core, and PCS,

**[02:17:57.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002693)  
or earmarks from 2022 to 2027

**[02:18:00.620] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002694)  
for their intended health purposes,

**[02:18:03.440] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002695)  
particularly to strengthen PhilHealth benefit packages

**[02:18:06.620] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002696)  
and reduce the burden of out-of-pocket spending.

**[02:18:10.300] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002697)  
Third, elimination of discretionary health patronage funds

**[02:18:14.600] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002698)  
such as MAIFIP.

**[02:18:16.400] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002699)  
We call for the transfer of discretionary medical assistance funds,

**[02:18:20.220] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002700)  
including MAIFIP, directly to PhilHealth

**[02:18:22.780] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002701)  
and their conversion into transparent, rules-based health benefits,

**[02:18:27.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002702)  
including effective zero-distance billing

**[02:18:29.780] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002703)  
for indigent and financially incapacitated patients.

**[02:18:33.480] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002704)  
And lastly, transparency and accountability.

**[02:18:36.120] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002705)  
We demand full public disclosure and accountability

**[02:18:38.500] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002706)  
for all executive releases and congressional budget actions

**[02:18:43.220] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002707)  
affecting universal healthcare,

**[02:18:45.960] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002708)  
including the allocation, withholding, release,

**[02:18:50.100] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002709)  
and utilization of funds intended for PhilHealth

**[02:18:52.920] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002710)  
and other UHC programs.

**[02:18:54.640] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002711)  
So every day, on the front lines of our healthcare system,

**[02:18:57.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002712)  
we witness the devastating human cost of these budget decisions.

**[02:19:08.900] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002713)  
As healthcare workers, we see the families of our patients

**[02:19:13.960] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002714)  
driven into deep poverty by catastrophic hospital bills.

**[02:19:17.220] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002715)  
We see patients delay or forego urgent treatment

**[02:19:19.780] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002716)  
because they cannot afford it.

**[02:19:21.840] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002717)  
We are forced to watch patients go without the funds

**[02:19:25.820] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002718)  
they need to receive the life-saving care.

**[02:19:27.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002719)  
And we see something that should never be acceptable

**[02:19:29.940] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002720)  
in a democratic society.

**[02:19:31.900] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002721)  
Filipinos are forced to beg, line up at politicians' offices,

**[02:19:36.520] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002722)  
and seek personal favor simply to obtain healthcare

**[02:19:39.260] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002723)  
that should be their right.

**[02:19:41.540] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002724)  
So this is not UHC.

**[02:19:45.160] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002725)  
Healthcare is the people's right.

**[02:19:47.200] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002726)  
And I'm sure all of us have the same sentiment

**[02:19:50.700] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002727)  
and recommendations.

**[02:19:52.040] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002728)  
It must never be reduced to a discretionary fund

**[02:19:54.900] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002729)  
nor converted into currency.

**[02:19:57.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002730)  
It must never be reduced to political patronage

**[02:19:58.860] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002731)  
or self-interest.

**[02:20:00.480] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002732)  
So kami po, ang mga miyembro po ng Healthcare Professional Alliance,

**[02:20:05.260] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002733)  
after COVID-19,

**[02:20:07.240] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002734)  
we call on Congress, the Executive,

**[02:20:09.940] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002735)  
and all those interested with the people's resources

**[02:20:12.220] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002736)  
to put patients and people, not politics,

**[02:20:16.120] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002737)  
at the center of the 2027 National Budget.

**[02:20:18.680] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002738)  
So marami pong salamat.

**[02:20:22.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002739)  
Thank you, Dr. Albert Ong.

**[02:20:24.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002740)  
Senator Laxon would like to make an interjection.

**[02:20:27.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002741)  
I'm not sure it escapes my mind.

**[02:20:29.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002742)  
Singit na lang ako.

**[02:20:30.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002743)  
I'm not sure kung na ng mga colleagues nyo yung

**[02:20:33.980] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002744)  
ano ba yung total accumulated unreleased earmarks?

**[02:20:39.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002745)  
Yung nag-accumulate na over the years.

**[02:20:41.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002746)  
Meron kayong data nyan?

**[02:20:43.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002747)  
Yung from PagCorp, PCSO,

**[02:20:47.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002748)  
yung mga earmarks sa Syntax, etc.

**[02:20:51.120] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002749)  
Based po sa estimate po sa group,

**[02:20:54.480] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002750)  
it's 311 billion.

**[02:20:56.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002751)  
Ganyan.

**[02:20:57.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002752)  
From what year?

**[02:20:58.780] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002753)  
Ilang years itong?

**[02:21:00.440] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002754)  
Basta total accumulated?

**[02:21:01.500] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-002755)  
Since 2022 po.

**[02:21:02.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002756)  
Since 2022.

**[02:21:04.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002757)  
Thank you.

**[02:21:05.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002758)  
Thank you, Senator Laxon.

**[02:21:07.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002759)  
Yes, Sir President Win.

**[02:21:09.720] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002760)  
Kasi yung topic is about itong PhilHealth.

**[02:21:15.860] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002761)  
And since si Atty. Pao is an attorney,

**[02:21:20.280] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002762)  
there's such a thing as soft earmarking and hard earmarking.

**[02:21:25.260] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002763)  
In fact, I was consulting our...

**[02:21:27.680] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002764)  
Lawyers at the back.

**[02:21:29.260] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002765)  
And during 2026, ito rin natanong ko,

**[02:21:32.160] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002766)  
bakit may soft earmark, hard earmark?

**[02:21:34.480] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002767)  
Earmark is an earmark, di ba?

**[02:21:36.520] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002768)  
But it's a legal definition under different laws.

**[02:21:44.360] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002769)  
Sabi nga ni Senator Laxon, maraming laws kasi nag-earmark.

**[02:21:47.760] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002770)  
Meron sa agri, meron sa sports, meron sa PhilHealth,

**[02:21:53.540] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002771)  
meron pa nga yung latest namin,

**[02:21:55.640] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002772)  
dinagay namin sa creatives.

**[02:21:57.500] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002773)  
But apparently, if you don't specify in the language

**[02:22:02.540] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002774)  
that it will go to the special account for general fund,

**[02:22:07.500] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002775)  
hindi siya ma-earmark talaga.

**[02:22:10.640] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002776)  
Ma-account lang siya, pero hindi siya ma-appropriate.

**[02:22:14.920] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002777)  
So ganyan nangyari sa universal healthcare.

**[02:22:18.320] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002778)  
Naiipon lang siya, alam natin kung magkano,

**[02:22:21.060] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002779)  
yung dapat pumunta.

**[02:22:22.720] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002780)  
Pero pagdating sa appropriations,

**[02:22:25.080] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002781)  
hindi siya in-appropriate.

**[02:22:26.540] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002782)  
So,

**[02:22:27.500] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002783)  
something that the health advocates might want to study,

**[02:22:32.840] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002784)  
because we might amend the law later on

**[02:22:37.180] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002785)  
para maging automatic na at malagay na talaga sa appropriations.

**[02:22:43.880] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002786)  
Kung hindi, na-account natin, alam natin 311 billion,

**[02:22:47.920] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002787)  
pero there's no law that will trigger the appropriations.

**[02:22:52.300] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002788)  
Kaya ganoon na nangyari.

**[02:22:57.500] Senate President Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-002789)  
Yes, go ahead, attorney.

**[02:22:59.080] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002790)  
Sir, if I may,

**[02:22:59.940] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002791)  
I think it's no longer a question of whether it's a soft or a hard earmark.

**[02:23:06.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002792)  
I think because the recent Supreme Court decision is clear naman

**[02:23:10.240] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002793)  
that this is a special fund for a special purpose.

**[02:23:12.620] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002794)  
And in that Supreme Court decision,

**[02:23:15.600] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002795)  
for transparency purposes, ako po yung lawyer doon.

**[02:23:18.060] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002796)  
At yung ibang members ng CSO dito ay mga petitioners in that case.

**[02:23:22.280] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002797)  
The Supreme Court actually discussed,

**[02:23:24.020] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002798)  
especially in the part of Justice Caguiwa,

**[02:23:25.880] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002799)  
discussing that even without the,

**[02:23:27.340] Action for Economic Reforms Representative Atty. Paula Mae B. Tanquieng** (asr-002800)

**[02:37:33.560] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003326)

**[02:37:35.400] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003327)

**[02:37:37.360] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003328)  
health budget. It is not

**[02:37:39.360] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003329)

**[02:37:41.500] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003330)  
on health, but how that

**[02:37:43.460] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003331)  
investment will shape the country's capacity

**[02:37:45.680] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003332)  
to prevent illness,

**[02:37:47.520] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003333)  
promote well-being, respond to

**[02:37:49.480] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003334)  
emerging risks, and provide care

**[02:37:51.520] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003335)  
when and where people actually

**[02:37:53.560] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003336)  
need it. Our first

**[02:37:55.380] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003337)  
point, health promotion is

**[02:37:57.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003338)  
part of health care. Health promotion

**[02:37:59.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003339)  
is a core component of a

**[02:38:01.420] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003340)  
functioning health system.

**[02:38:03.240] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003341)  
Even the WHO defines health

**[02:38:05.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003342)  
promotion as enabling people to

**[02:38:07.260] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003343)  
increase control over and improve

**[02:38:09.500] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003344)  
their health. It includes

**[02:38:11.120] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003345)  
strengthening health literacy, creating

**[02:38:13.320] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003346)  
supportive environments,

**[02:38:15.440] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003347)  
enabling healthier choices,

**[02:38:17.360] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003348)  
addressing social determinants

**[02:38:19.620] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003349)  
of health, and helping

**[02:38:21.060] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003350)  
communities participate meaningfully

**[02:38:23.100] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003351)  
in decisions that affect our

**[02:38:25.240] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003352)  
well-being. For this reason,

**[02:38:27.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003353)  
health promotion should not be treated

**[02:38:29.460] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003354)  
as an optional layer

**[02:38:31.220] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003355)  
of the health system that can

**[02:38:33.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003356)  
be substantially reduced whenever

**[02:38:35.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003357)  
resources become constrained.

**[02:38:37.800] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003358)  
Kaya hindi po namin maintindihan

**[02:38:39.660] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003359)  
kung bakit nabawasan

**[02:38:41.420] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003360)  
ang health promotion budget ng

**[02:38:43.280] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003361)  
DOH from approximately

**[02:38:45.200] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003362)  
2.5 billion

**[02:38:47.000] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003363)  
to 490 million

**[02:38:49.220] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003364)  
sa house version, a

**[02:38:51.240] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003365)  
reduction of roughly 81%.

**[02:38:54.280] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003366)  
Ang tanong

**[02:38:55.420] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003367)  
po namin ay, what

**[02:38:57.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003368)  
evidence, criteria, and assessment

**[02:38:59.300] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003369)  
inform such a substantial

**[02:39:01.260] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003370)  
reduction in health promotion funding

**[02:39:03.360] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003371)  
and what programs, communities,

**[02:39:05.740] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003372)  
and populations could be

**[02:39:07.240] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003373)  
affected if this reduction

**[02:39:09.120] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003374)  
is retained? That question

**[02:39:11.100] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003375)  
deserves a clear answer.

**[02:39:13.380] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003376)  
Our second point, prevention

**[02:39:15.220] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003377)  
and treatment must work together.

**[02:39:17.760] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003378)  
We recognize the importance

**[02:39:19.140] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003379)  
of medical assistance for Filipinos

**[02:39:21.360] Youth for Mental Health Coalition Representative Ray Alyannah Ysabel Lagasca** (asr-003380)  
who cannot afford health care.

**[03:08:39.040] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004080)

**[03:08:49.660] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004083)

**[03:09:10.900] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004087)

**[03:09:14.940] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004088)

**[03:09:20.900] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004090)

**[03:09:27.580] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004091)

**[03:09:30.000] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004092)  
And of course, these commitments were further operationalized

**[03:09:33.180] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004093)  
through the Philippine Strategic Plan against OSAIC and SISAEM 2025 to 2028,

**[03:09:38.060] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004094)  
launched naman po in December 2025.

**[03:09:41.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004095)  
So, to support the implementation, the NCC OSAIC-SISAEM,

**[03:09:45.520] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004096)  
Social Watch Philippines, alongside other member agencies,

**[03:09:49.380] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004097)  
and CSOs, developed the Costed Implementation Plan,

**[03:09:53.480] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004098)  
translating priorities into time-bound activities,

**[03:09:57.020] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004099)  
resource requirements, and cost estimates,

**[03:09:59.140] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004100)  
aligned with the national budgeting cycle.

**[03:10:02.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004101)  
So, with this framework po, kami po sa Social Watch Philippines Alternative Budget Initiative

**[03:10:07.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004102)  
Child Protection Cluster na binubo po ng iba't-ibang child-focused organizations.

**[03:10:13.780] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004103)  
Isa po ako doon from World Vision, and of course, yung iba po pong kasama ay nandito din.

**[03:10:18.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004104)  
We propose,

**[03:10:19.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004105)  
We propose an alternative budget of 4.4 billion peso for fiscal year 2027

**[03:10:24.660] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004106)  
to strengthen the national child protection system.

**[03:10:28.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004107)  
So, this includes dedicated allocations for the OSAIC-SISAEM,

**[03:10:32.600] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004108)  
proper proposals across key result areas,

**[03:10:35.500] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004109)  
to fund advocacy, case management, law enforcement databases,

**[03:10:39.800] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004110)  
and specialized training, amounting to 154 million pesos.

**[03:10:44.600] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004111)  
So, ang mga beneficiaries po nito ay mga child victim survivors,

**[03:10:49.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004112)  
and adolescent survivors of sexual abuse, physical abuse, exploitation,

**[03:10:54.220] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004113)  
trafficking, and OSAIC and SISAEM,

**[03:10:56.100] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004114)  
as well as children requiring trauma-informed mental health care.

**[03:11:01.040] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004115)  
The proposals also emphasize meaningful child and youth participation

**[03:11:05.020] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004116)  
in prevention and response,

**[03:11:07.580] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004117)  
ensuring the solutions are child-friendly, gender-sensitive, and inclusive.

**[03:11:13.200] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004118)  
If mapaflash po yung aming presentation on this,

**[03:11:17.000] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004119)  
meron po kaming sinabmit na,

**[03:11:19.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004120)  
PowerPoint for our proposed budget proposals.

**[03:11:23.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004121)  
But, I'll go through na lang po,

**[03:11:25.140] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004122)  
and hopefully may flash din po siya later,

**[03:11:27.640] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004123)  
or kung pwede na po ngayon.

**[03:11:29.980] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004124)  
So, for yung aming po proposal,

**[03:11:31.860] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004125)  
ay para sa iba't-ibang member agencies po ng NCCO-SAEC-SISAEM.

**[03:11:36.320] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004126)  
So, we'll start po with DOJ, NCCO-SAEC Secretariat.

**[03:11:42.760] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004127)  
We're proposing po a total of,

**[03:11:49.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004134)  
105 million pesos.

**[03:11:52.260] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004135)  
So kasama po doon yung case management and first contact protocols

**[03:11:56.880] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004136)  
that will help standardize how frontline responders handle osaig cases

**[03:12:01.620] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004137)  
from initial contact and rescue operations to referral for appropriate care.

**[03:12:07.900] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004138)  
Importante din po dito yung 90 million pesos for an integrated database and registry system.

**[03:12:14.080] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004139)  
So this one po, the proposed system aims to centralize and harmonize relevant data

**[03:12:19.960] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004140)  
to support offender identification and law enforcement investigations,

**[03:12:24.780] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004141)  
as well as an additional 4 million for dedicated technical personnel

**[03:12:28.220] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004142)  
to manage and analyze cyber tips, maintain data quality,

**[03:12:32.740] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004143)  
and turn information into actionable investigative leads.

**[03:12:37.380] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004144)  
We're also proposing po yung monitoring and evaluation,

**[03:12:42.040] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004145)  
joint trainings,

**[03:12:43.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004146)  
that will help frontline personnel standardize how they recognize,

**[03:12:47.800] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004147)  
report, record, and refer osaig sisaing cases.

**[03:12:52.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004148)  
Establishment of dedicated media and public relations bureau

**[03:12:57.560] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004149)  
and media partnership forum.

**[03:13:00.600] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004150)  
For DepEd po, we are proposing capacity building activities.

**[03:13:06.360] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004151)  
Kasama po dito yung youth peer-to-peer support facilitator training

**[03:13:10.140] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004152)  
and training of educators and non-teaching personnel.

**[03:13:13.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004153)  
In osaig and sisaing hotspot areas.

**[03:13:16.900] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004154)  
For Department of Health,

**[03:13:19.760] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004155)  
ito po yung medyo may malaking proposal.

**[03:13:24.200] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004156)  
And we have been proposing this for years.

**[03:13:26.200] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004157)  
And we'd like to emphasize this again,

**[03:13:28.460] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004158)  
which is the 145 million for dedicated multidisciplinary teams

**[03:13:33.020] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004159)  
in women and children protection units across 38 DOH retained hospitals.

**[03:13:38.680] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004160)  
So we emphasize the importance po of,

**[03:13:43.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004161)  
having a dedicated team including physicians, psychologists,

**[03:13:48.040] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004162)  
nurse and medical social worker providing coordinated medical psychosocial

**[03:13:53.240] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004163)  
and social work support alongside appropriate legal referrals and follow-up care.

**[03:13:59.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004164)  
For DSWD po, we are proposing a total of 28.56 million,

**[03:14:06.100] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004165)  
which covers basic course on anti-osaig that aims to strengthen foundational competencies of social workers,

**[03:14:14.000] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004166)  
and case managers.

**[03:14:15.900] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004167)  
Ayan po yung aming presentation.

**[03:14:17.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004168)  
Thank you po for sharing.

**[03:14:20.700] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004169)  
Ayan po, sa DSWD kasama din po doon yung project safe capacity building,

**[03:14:25.200] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004170)  
establishment and enhancement of four child advocacy centers

**[03:14:31.500] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004171)  
and joint wellness programs for frontline officers.

**[03:14:35.300] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004172)  
Next po is for Council for the Welfare of Children.

**[03:14:40.640] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004173)  
Dito po, big bulk dito ay,

**[03:14:42.940] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004174)  
focus po of child participation po,

**[03:14:46.940] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004175)  
kasama po yung mga bata sa pagbubuo ng IEC materials,

**[03:14:50.940] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004176)  
paggawa ng AdvoCom plan and SBCC planning workshop with children and adult,

**[03:14:56.440] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004177)  
and yung pag-rollout po ng Makabata Helpline 1383.

**[03:15:00.000] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004178)  
Makaimportante po nito dahil yung Makabata Helpline 1383 po ang ito po yung it aims to improve awareness of the helpline as a reporting and referral channel and connect families and communities to appropriate services.

**[03:15:18.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004179)  
Dahil nung nagkaroon po kami ng mga consultations, ang sinasabi po ng mga bata ay importante po sa kanila na meron silang mapagre-reportan or mapagsusumbungan in case na meron pong mga victims ng abuse, exploitation, and violence.

**[03:15:36.140] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004180)  
Next po is for DILG, NACC, and NCIP, a total combined amount of 3.6 million po which includes improved systems.

**[03:15:48.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004181)  
and protocols for OSAEM and SISAEC case identification, referral management, and monitoring.

**[03:15:55.400] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004182)  
So yung pag-train po ng mga LGUs on case management and referral system.

**[03:15:59.840] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004183)  
Users training on ASO for NACC and Maraco and capacity building po for IP communities including children po na makasama po sila sa capacity building on improved capacity.

**[03:16:18.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004184)  
Next po, ito po yung summary lang po nung aming mga proposals.

**[03:16:27.240] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004185)  
So ito po ay katuwang po ng mga agencies na kasama po namin sa NCC OSAEC-SISAEM and we've already shared this with them as well po.

**[03:16:36.300] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004186)  
Yung sa tingin po namin na dapat i-prioritize dahil po yung ating strategic plan ay until 2028 na lang po.

**[03:16:42.960] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004187)  
And we're hoping na by 2027 ay magkaroon po ng sapat na pono.

**[03:16:48.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004188)  
Para magawa po itong ating mga PPAs under the strategic plan.

**[03:16:54.680] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004189)  
So next po is yung, ito po meron po kaming 5 PPAs outside ng costed implementation plan na we think are also important po to include sa ating budget.

**[03:17:05.880] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004190)  
So beyond the costed implementation plan, we think that these are essential to address child protection and mental health needs.

**[03:17:14.760] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004191)  
Yung ilan po dito, I think na-mention na rin po kanina.

**[03:17:18.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004192)  
So i-re-reiterate ko na lang po siya.

**[03:17:20.680] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004193)  
So in child-friendly and accessible mental health support service amounting to 4 billion.

**[03:17:26.640] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004194)  
Kasama din po yung nabanggit ko na kanina na women and children protection units in 38 DOH-retained hospitals amounting to 145 million plus.

**[03:17:37.440] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004195)  
And then ito po ay budget restoration of the NCC against OSAEC-SISAEM.

**[03:17:43.020] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004196)  
And sinare din po sa amin ng sekretaryat na may sinentong letter dito.

**[03:17:48.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004197)  
May visa office po ninyo for the request to restore po yung kanila pong budget from 2026.

**[03:17:55.540] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004198)  
So additional 70 million po yun.

**[03:17:58.220] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004199)  
Of course, budget restoration and augmentation po ng child protection program sa DepEd naman po ito.

**[03:18:04.340] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004200)  
Na maibalik po yung budget line item na child protection program.

**[03:18:09.700] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004201)  
Kasi po sa ngayon ay nasubsume na po siya sa learner support program nila.

**[03:18:14.620] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004202)  
And ang amount po din is 44 million.

**[03:18:18.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004203)  
Ito din po wish list ng 68 additional plantelia positions for the NCC-OSAEC-SISAEM sekretaryat amounting to 41 million pesos.

**[03:18:27.680] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004204)  
So ayan po yung summary po yung kabuuan po nung aming pong request na 4.4 billion pesos po.

**[03:18:35.820] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004205)  
So I'd like to end my presentation po by saying po again na ang bata ang bida sa budget at pera ng taong bayan ay dapat para sa taong bayan.

**[03:18:44.660] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004206)  
So sana po maisip niyo na ito ay wish list.

**[03:18:48.120] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004207)  
Hindi lamang ng mga nakakatanda pero ng mga bata din po lalo na po ngayong Pasko.

**[03:18:52.780] World Vision Advocacy Specialist Pauline Navarro** (asr-004208)  
Thank you.

**[03:18:54.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004209)  
Thank you.

**[03:18:55.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004210)  
Next is sa Unang Hakbang Foundation.

**[03:18:58.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004211)  
Miss Mary Ayin Padoga.

**[03:19:01.680] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004212)  
Ayin po.

**[03:19:02.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004213)  
Ayin.

**[03:19:03.620] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004214)  
Yes po.

**[03:19:04.520] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004215)  
Good afternoon everyone.

**[03:19:05.820] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004216)  
Good afternoon po honorable senators who are here as well as all the CSOs po nandito.

**[03:19:11.060] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004217)  
So Unang Hakbang is an operating foundation that provides remedial classes in reading math.

**[03:19:18.120] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004218)  
For children at risk of dropping out of school, living in the poorest or poorer sections of Mandaluyong and Pampanga.

**[03:19:25.540] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004219)  
Issues that confront us daily and which impact how children learn are hunger, malnourished, lack of water and proper sanitation and inability of parents to support their children's learning.

**[03:19:38.500] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004220)  
These same issues are among those that have limited country's progress on the 2030 Sustainable Development Goals.

**[03:19:48.120] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004221)  
So ang sa Unang Hakbang lang po ay magbibigay lang po kami ng tatlong recommendations for this session.

**[03:20:01.780] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004222)  
So yung una po ay yung hunger and malnutrition.

**[03:20:04.500] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004223)  
So we recommend na sana po ay may restoration of 26 budget of dev ed for school based feeding program.

**[03:20:14.860] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004224)  
Or to 26 billion from 11.4 billion.

**[03:20:18.120] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004225)  
As now proposed.

**[03:20:19.460] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004226)  
Yung pangalawa naman po ay yung provision of 300 million in DA budget for the research and initial implementation of a nutrition sensitive agricultural program and an agricultural supply chain network centered on bringing produce to areas near families and schools.

**[03:20:41.500] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004227)  
Yung pangalawang issue naman po ay yung bullying and learners mental health.

**[03:20:45.480] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004228)  
So narinig po natin no?

**[03:20:47.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004229)  
Hindi lang po talaga kumbaga pabata ng pabata ang may issue or usapin na patungkol sa mental health.

**[03:20:56.580] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004230)  
So hindi ko na i-explain why kasi it's really issues that we are really encounter right now.

**[03:21:08.120] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004231)  
So yung recommendation lang po namin is the provision of 300 million for learners welfare intended to strengthen child protection.

**[03:21:17.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004232)  
And the training of all teachers and students to recognize peers who might need help.

**[03:21:24.440] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004233)  
The aim is to initially address the need of the 10,000 that EDCOM 2 has identified as lacking even an anti-bullying policy.

**[03:21:34.660] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004234)  
And another one is the provision of another 300 million to support inset training on a positive parenting in schools to create a more just in a peaceful environment in campuses.

**[03:21:47.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004235)  
And another issues naman po ay yun nga hindi din matutulungan ang mga bata kung hindi din natin i-train or hindi tayo sama-sama para magkaroon ng peaceful environment ang mga bata sa Pilipinas.

**[03:22:02.420] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004236)  
So ito naman po ay nag-focus sa SDG 4, SDG 5, and SDG 16 ay ang yung adult education and lifelong learning.

**[03:22:11.820] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004237)  
So ito it's all in its house EST or education and skill training.

**[03:22:17.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004238)  
And for implementers, DepEd reporting that 2017, there were at least 19 million adults or 18 to 59 years old, comprising a third of that age group who had not completed their basic education.

**[03:22:33.240] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004239)  
In the quality basic education development plan, on DepEd underscored that need the strong collaboration involving all education stakeholders from national institutions.

**[03:22:47.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004240)  
From public to central, public to private, and most notably teachers, parents, and children.

**[03:22:53.260] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004241)  
The entire nation must work together to deliver better quality basic education for all and contribute to inclusive economic growth in the Philippines.

**[03:23:02.340] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004242)  
So through that, our recommendation is the restoration of the line item for community learning centers to house alts and lifelong learning classes.

**[03:23:14.320] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004243)  
And also annual allocation.

**[03:23:17.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004244)  
For the construction of CLCs, 200 million until all municipalities have at least one CLC.

**[03:23:25.880] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004245)  
Specified that local government support fund or LGSF may be also used to finance the construction of CLCs and the training of women as para-counselors to support effort to eliminate bullying and improve the mental health of students.

**[03:23:42.380] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004246)  
And 100 million honoraria for volunteers who teach in CLCs.

**[03:23:47.140] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004247)  
And the CLCs also take notice not just for learning, this is also a safe space for children, for youth, and even for the parents.

**[03:23:58.020] Unang Hakbang Foundation Program Manager Mary Aieen Padoga** (asr-004248)  
So I think that's all po. Thank you.

**[03:24:00.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004249)  
Thank you.
