Sen. Fanpilo Lacson, Co-Chair ng Finance Committee.

**[03:24:31.680] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004254)  
At ang ating Senate President, Wyn Gatchalian.

**[03:24:37.300] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004255)  
At mga kasama sa Civil Society Organizations.

**[03:24:42.060] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004256)  
At maging ang aking kasama rin sa INET Philippines.

**[03:24:47.140] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004257)  
Na walang iba, kundi ang aming Advocacy Officer, Mercy Chan.

**[03:24:51.860] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004258)  
Magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[03:24:54.540] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004259)  
Sen. J.B. Batang San Juan.

**[03:25:00.700] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004260)  
Ang Education Network for INET Philippines.

**[03:25:07.640] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004261)  
Isang pambansang koalisyon na binubuo po ng 100 organizations.

**[03:25:13.780] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004262)  
More than 100 organizations nationwide.

**[03:25:16.040] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004263)  
Advocating for inclusive, equitable, quality education for all.

**[03:25:21.000] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004264)  
At siya po ang lead organizations education cluster ng Alternative Budget Initiative under Social Watch Philippines.

**[03:25:31.480] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004265)  
At nagpapasalamat po kami sa Senado for this opportunity to present our recommendations on the House-approved FY2027 General Appropriations Bill.

**[03:25:44.080] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004266)  
Our central message is,

**[03:25:46.040] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004267)  
education cannot be declared a national priority while essential programs remain unfunded.

**[03:25:55.080] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004268)  
Legal mandates lack adequate resources and marginalized learners continue to be left behind.

**[03:26:02.860] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004269)  
While the House government maintains a substantial allocation for education,

**[03:26:07.700] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004270)  
what matters is not only how much government spends,

**[03:26:12.000] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004271)  
but where resources go, who benefit,

**[03:26:16.040] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004272)  
and whether the budget addresses persistent inequalities in education.

**[03:26:22.540] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004273)  
Based on our review of the House GAAP and INET's Alternative Budget Proposal,

**[03:26:28.720] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004274)  
we urge the Senate to address four priorities.

**[03:26:34.000] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004275)  
First, restore and expand funding for inclusive education and lifelong learning.

**[03:26:41.420] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004276)  
Ang krisis edukasyon na hindi lamang tumutukoy.

**[03:26:45.400] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004277)  
Sa mga batang nasa loob ng paaralan,

**[03:26:49.080] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004278)  
kundi sa mahigit na sampung milyon na mga bata at kabataan na wala sa paaralan.

**[03:26:55.900] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004279)  
At gayon din, mahigit sa labing siyam na milyon adults who missed out of education.

**[03:27:04.780] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004280)  
Ang panawagan pong ito namin, natin, dapat po'y walang naiiwan.

**[03:27:11.980] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004281)  
No one left behind.

**[03:27:15.400] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004282)  
We acknowledge the House increases for special needs education in SNED,

**[03:27:22.400] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004283)  
indigenous people education, and madrasa education.

**[03:27:25.480] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004284)  
However, these allocations remain below their FY2026 levels and fall short of what is needed.

**[03:27:35.960] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004285)  
INET proposes P2.393 billion for SNED.

**[03:27:40.060] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004286)  
Kung titignan po natin, noong 2026, it's P1.040.

**[03:27:45.400] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004287)  
P2.393 billion na bawasan to support schools, operationalize inclusive learning resource centers,

**[03:27:53.260] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004288)  
strengthen child find, and provide accessible learning materials and support services.

**[03:27:58.940] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004289)  
For the alternative learning system and lifelong learning,

**[03:28:02.880] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004290)  
the House GAAP retains P3.849 billion under the broader Flexible Learning Options Program.

**[03:28:11.160] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004291)  
But, a dedicated ALS allocation,

**[03:28:15.020] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004294)  
remains unclear.

**[03:28:17.560] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004295)  
E-Net proposes P5.55 billion to expand ALS access,

**[03:28:23.680] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004296)  
flexible learning,

**[03:28:25.680] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004297)  
for working adults and community learning centers,

**[03:28:28.760] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004298)  
especially in underserved areas.

**[03:28:31.560] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004299)  
We call for P5 billion in dedicated funding for last mile and GHIDA schools

**[03:28:37.620] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004300)  
and adequate support for indigenous and Madrasa education.

**[03:28:43.480] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004301)  
We urge the Senate

**[03:28:44.280] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004302)  
We urge the Senate

**[03:28:45.020] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004303)  
protect, and effectively implement funding for the ARAL program

**[03:28:50.480] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004304)  
and appropriations must translate into actual tutoring and learning recovery

**[03:28:55.620] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004305)  
for struggling learners with funded tutors,

**[03:28:59.700] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004306)  
clear implementation targets,

**[03:29:02.120] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004307)  
and transparent reporting on delivery and results.

**[03:29:06.220] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004308)  
So, our second proposal,

**[03:29:09.660] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004309)  
restore and strengthen program-supporting learners' nutrition,

**[03:29:13.180] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004310)  
protection, and well-being.

**[03:29:16.520] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004311)  
The House GAAP retains 11.431 billion for the screening program,

**[03:29:24.600] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004312)  
substantially below the 25.695 billion allocation in FY2026.

**[03:29:33.700] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004313)  
ENET proposes 42.295 billion to restore existing funding,

**[03:29:40.220] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004314)  
expand feeding to 2 million additional learners,

**[03:29:43.180] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004315)  
and strengthen program delivery.

**[03:29:47.960] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004316)  
And then, we call for dedicated funding for comprehensive school safety,

**[03:29:55.620] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004317)  
child protection, school mental health, and education in emergencies.

**[03:30:00.000] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004318)  
Beyond physical security, schools need prevention and response system for bullying, violence, abuse, discrimination, and disasters,

**[03:30:11.300] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004319)  
accessible reporting and referral mechanisms, trained personnel, and psychosocial support.

**[03:30:17.980] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004320)  
Funding must support a whole school, inclusive and rights-based approach, not merely new mandates without implementation resources.

**[03:30:27.580] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004321)  
Third, adequately fund teachers' welfare, school personnel, and inclusive teaching.

**[03:30:34.800] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004322)  
We urge the Senate to address continuing shortages of teachers, school principals, and administrative personnel.

**[03:30:42.380] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004323)  
ENET proposes funding for 14,274 additional teaching positions, additional principals, and administrative officers,

**[03:30:52.380] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004324)  
and 4.8 million for teaching overload compensation.

**[03:30:56.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004325)  
We also call for full funding of the Magna Carta benefits, including special hardship allowances,

**[03:31:04.860] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004326)  
transfer and deployment support, and teachers' mental health services.

**[03:31:10.260] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004327)  
Actually, RA 4670 is still in problem.

**[03:31:17.800] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004328)  
The correct implementation and full implementation of the Magna Carta.

**[03:31:23.000] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004329)  
Senator Ping and Chair.

**[03:31:26.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004330)  
No, we would like to ask your support for this.

**[03:31:29.660] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004331)  
Further, we propose 340 million for positive discipline in everyday teaching with disability inclusion perspective.

**[03:31:40.300] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004332)  
Training for 100,000 teachers initially to strengthen positive discipline and inclusive disability responsive teaching.

**[03:31:50.240] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004333)  
Quality education requires teachers whose rights are respected, well-protected, and professionalized.

**[03:31:56.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004334)  
Educational needs adequately supported.

**[03:31:59.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004335)  
Fourth, strengthen financing for early childhood education and underserved regions, particularly BARM.

**[03:32:06.300] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004336)  
We call for adequate investments in supplementary feeding, child development centers,

**[03:32:11.920] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004337)  
and professionalization and fair compensation of child development workers and teachers.

**[03:32:17.460] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004338)  
For BARM, we propose targeted investments in ALS community learning centers,

**[03:32:22.700] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004339)  
mobile teachers, early childhood facilities,

**[03:32:26.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004340)  
and learning recovery with resources directed to the most disadvantaged and conflict-affected communities.

**[03:32:33.380] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004341)  
Finally, we urge the Senate to ensure transparency, accountability, and equity in budget realignments.

**[03:32:41.860] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004342)  
Low fund utilization should not automatically be equated with low demand or divinish need.

**[03:32:48.600] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004343)  
It may reflect procurement delays, inadequate institutional capacity, and implementation bottlenecks

**[03:32:55.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004344)  
that governments may face.

**[03:32:56.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004345)  
The government must address, not use as justification for reducing essential education allocations.

**[03:33:03.860] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004346)  
We therefore urge the Senate to scrutinize house budget realignments,

**[03:33:09.180] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004347)  
ensure clear program-level allocations, and protect resources for marginalized learners.

**[03:33:16.020] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004348)  
Honorable Senators,

**[03:33:18.320] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004349)  
INET Philippines calls on the Senate

**[03:33:21.380] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004350)  
1. to restore cuts to essential education programs,

**[03:33:26.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004351)  
expand investments or address persistent education inequalities,

**[03:33:32.220] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004352)  
earmark and fund the mandates.

**[03:33:34.920] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004353)  
Kung kailan marami tayong mga batas na naipasa, bumaba ang budget.

**[03:33:40.660] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004354)  
Halimbawa lang po ng mga mandates natin.

**[03:33:43.320] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004355)  
RA 11510, Alternative Learning System Act.

**[03:33:48.360] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004356)  
11650, Inclusive Education Act.

**[03:33:51.180] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004357)  
RA 12313, Lifelong Learning Development Framework.

**[03:33:56.800] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004358)  
And then, 12321, Last Mile Schools Act.

**[03:34:00.680] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004359)  
And RA 4670, Magna Karta for Public School Teachers.

**[03:34:06.400] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004360)  
Protect education equity by prioritizing learners and communities facing the greatest barriers.

**[03:34:16.040] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004361)  
We respectfully submit our detailed alternative budget proposals for consideration in the Senate's amendment to the FY2027 GAP.

**[03:34:26.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004362)  
The challenge is not simply to maintain a large education budget,

**[03:34:29.940] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004363)  
but to ensure that every peso advances the right to inclusive, equitable, and quality education for all,

**[03:34:38.280] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004364)  
especially those who have been historically underserved and left behind.

**[03:34:43.800] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004365)  
Kaya nga po, pondo para sa karapatan ng mga mag-aaral sa edukasyon at edukasyon para sa lahat.

**[03:34:51.240] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004366)  
Mula sa INET Philippines, Education Network Flora Arillano,

**[03:34:56.200] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004367)  
presidente ng organisasyon na ito.

**[03:35:00.020] E-Net Philippines National Executive Board Member Prof. Flora C. Arellano** (asr-004368)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:35:01.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004369)  
Maraming salamat po, Professor Flora Arillano.

**[03:35:06.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004370)  
Next is Mr. Humphrey Soriano from Center for Youth Advocacy.

**[03:35:15.980] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004371)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chairperson and Honorable Members of the Committee.

**[03:35:19.880] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004372)  
Along with the CSOs, maraming salamat po sa oportunidad para makapagsalita.

**[03:35:24.600] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004373)  
Ako po si Humphrey Soriano.

**[03:35:26.200] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004374)  
Sa Amplify Education Budget Initiative,

**[03:35:28.100] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004375)  
the Budget Campaign of Multiply-Ed Philippines,

**[03:35:30.700] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004376)  
ito po ay proyekto ng Center for Youth Advocacy and Networking at Government Watch,

**[03:35:34.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004377)  
supported by Education Out Loud ng Global Partnership for Education.

**[03:35:38.880] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004378)  
Ang aming budget initiative po ay ang tanging youth-led education budget initiative in the country.

**[03:35:44.020] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004379)  
Simple lang po ang panawagan namin ngayong taon para sa education budget,

**[03:35:47.620] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004380)  
fix the gaps, fund the margins, and push for an inclusive education budget.

**[03:35:52.660] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004381)  
Ang kaibahan po namin, we have capacitated,

**[03:35:55.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004382)  
youth monitors all around the country.

**[03:35:58.040] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004383)  
We are present in nine areas all around the Philippines.

**[03:36:00.860] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004384)  
And nakita namin sa citizen monitoring namin na meron pa rin tayong kinakaharap na kisi sa edukasyon.

**[03:36:07.400] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004385)  
The House approved 987 billion pesos sa new appropriations for DepEd's budget.

**[03:36:13.060] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004386)  
Ito po ay mas bababa ng 6 billion pesos kesa sa kanilang proposal

**[03:36:16.280] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004387)  
at 50 billion pesos na mas bababa sa 2026 budget.

**[03:36:20.620] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004388)  
Karamihan po dito ay nanggaling sa cut on laptops in classrooms.

**[03:36:25.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004390)  
Ilan po sa mga agenda namin ay ang una, raise the alarm.

**[03:36:30.560] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004391)  
Hindi po pareho ang guard sa harap ng school at guidance counselor sa loob ng mga paaralan.

**[03:36:36.000] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004392)  
Mental health and child protection in the NEP,

**[03:36:38.780] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004393)  
in the NEP, both the Mental Health and Guidance Counseling Program and Child Protection

**[03:36:43.100] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004394)  
carry no dedicated or trackable line item.

**[03:36:46.180] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004395)  
Every expense category of the Child Protection Program now reads zero.

**[03:36:50.300] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004396)  
The House version did not change this.

**[03:36:53.000] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004397)  
Sa kabilang banda, 500 million peso,

**[03:36:55.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004398)  
is allotted for school safety and security.

**[03:36:58.700] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004399)  
We welcome it, but it's a special provision for security personnel.

**[03:37:02.580] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004400)  
A guard at the gate is not a counselor in the guidance office.

**[03:37:06.200] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004401)  
Sa kasalukuyan, mahigit 2,000 new counselor positions po

**[03:37:10.640] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004402)  
ang nasa loob ng new school personnel,

**[03:37:12.780] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004403)  
which is still 25% lower than in 2026.

**[03:37:17.100] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004404)  
On disaster resiliency,

**[03:37:19.120] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004405)  
our schools report full compliance on having a DRRM coordinator,

**[03:37:23.160] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004406)  
yet only 24% ng mga...

**[03:37:25.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004407)  
mga schools na na-monitor natin ay may bagong classroom

**[03:37:28.560] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004408)  
or naipagawa ang mga ito.

**[03:37:30.580] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004409)  
The House kept the 3 billion peso QRF under the same slow centralized rules

**[03:37:34.980] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004410)  
and cut the Safer and Resilient Schools Project by another 944 million pesos.

**[03:37:41.160] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004411)  
Idagdag na rin natin dito ang malabong 4-H policy ng DepEd

**[03:37:44.840] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004412)  
kung saan iniwan na naman sa mga paaralan

**[03:37:47.180] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004413)  
kung paano itutuloy ang disaster resilience and preparedness.

**[03:37:52.580] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004414)  
Transparency in the Classroom Fund.

**[03:37:55.100] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004415)  
The House cut...

**[03:37:55.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004416)  
basic education facilities by a further 3.10 billion pesos

**[03:37:59.400] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004417)

**[03:38:02.240] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004418)  
against the 85 billion pesos in 2026.

**[03:38:06.320] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004419)  
Sa loob po ng Special Provision No. 5,

**[03:38:08.740] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004420)

**[03:38:10.780] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004421)  
from last-mile schools to inclusive learning resource center.

**[03:38:14.640] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004422)  
Ang stance po namin,

**[03:38:16.080] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004423)

**[03:38:18.480] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004424)

**[03:38:21.420] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004425)

**[03:38:25.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004426)  
Matagal na rin namin hinihingi sa Department of Education

**[03:38:28.480] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004427)

**[03:38:31.960] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004428)  
Pero taon-taon, nagtuturuan kung kanino hinihingi ng allocation list.

**[03:38:36.420] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004429)  
Para po sa aming mga citizen monitor,

**[03:38:38.180] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004430)

**[03:38:42.020] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004431)  
or building sa ating mga paaralan.

**[03:38:44.520] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004432)  
On laptops, the computerization program was already reduced in the NEP.

**[03:38:50.020] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004433)  
Binawasan na naman ng house ito by 3.16 billion pesos.

**[03:38:55.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004434)  
The last-mile schools,

**[03:38:57.700] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004435)

**[03:39:00.320] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004436)  
ang Marofinas,

**[03:39:01.460] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004437)  
ang nasubukang mag-classroom sa isang covered court.

**[03:39:05.660] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004438)  
Ngayon, meron na sa ilang naipatay yung classroom

**[03:39:08.180] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004439)  
pero sa tulong ng mga civil society organization.

**[03:39:11.460] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004440)  
Ibig sabihin ito,

**[03:39:12.840] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004441)  
nakakalimutan pa rin natin ang mga last-mile school

**[03:39:15.460] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004442)  
sa kabila ng pagkakapasa ng Republic Act 12321

**[03:39:19.440] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004443)  
na nagre-require ng dedicated funding sa ating GAA.

**[03:39:23.260] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004444)  
At sa tumataas na kaso ng school-based fees,

**[03:39:25.940] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004445)  
ay sa tumataas na kaso ng stunting,

**[03:39:28.600] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004446)  
sa isa kami ng Inet Philippines sa pagsuporta

**[03:39:31.000] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004447)  
sa dagdag na budget sa school-based feeding program.

**[03:39:34.300] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004448)  
Isa pang magandang tingnan ang Wash and Schools program

**[03:39:37.140] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004449)  
kung saan sinabi ng mga studyante namin sa Bulacan

**[03:39:40.800] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004450)  
ang salitang namamahu na po kami.

**[03:39:44.960] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004451)  
Nakakatawa, pero

**[03:39:46.440] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004452)  
ang isang araw na pag-absent sa paaralan

**[03:39:49.980] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004453)  
dahil walang malinis na tubig

**[03:39:51.580] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004454)  
ay almost one year of learning loss

**[03:39:55.460] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004455)  
mula sa pandemic.

**[03:39:57.560] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004456)  
Sa inclusive education program,

**[03:40:00.000] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004457)  
natutuwa tayo na may dagdag

**[03:40:01.520] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004458)  
sa SNED, IPED, at Madrasa,

**[03:40:04.780] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004459)  
pero kailangan pa itong dagdagad.

**[03:40:07.320] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004460)  
We do not only point to the gaps,

**[03:40:09.020] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004461)  
we have costed the fix.

**[03:40:12.400] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004462)  
Masaya po kami i-share ang aming cost estimates

**[03:40:14.960] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004463)  
na gawa ng aming mga kabataan at youth organizations.

**[03:40:18.400] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004464)  
And funding a program is not the same as delivery.

**[03:40:21.520] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004465)  
Sinuling po namin na sana sa budget ng DepEd

**[03:40:24.000] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004466)  
ay mas i-decentralize

**[03:40:25.460] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004467)  
ang pagbibigay ng mga budget

**[03:40:27.860] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004468)  
at pabilisay ng procurement.

**[03:40:30.400] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004469)  
Mr. Chairperson and members of this committee,

**[03:40:33.400] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004470)  
Special Provision No. 5 allows DepEd

**[03:40:35.680] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004471)  
to partner with civil society.

**[03:40:37.700] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004472)  
Senior high school pa lamang po ako

**[03:40:39.240] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004473)  
nung nag-start ako sa budget advocacy

**[03:40:41.040] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004474)  
at nakita natin na kaya ng mga kabataan.

**[03:40:44.680] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004475)  
Our youth monitors are ready to help track

**[03:40:46.680] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004476)  
every classroom and every peso

**[03:40:48.660] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004477)  
from allocation to delivery,

**[03:40:50.820] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004478)  
fix the gaps, fund the margins

**[03:40:52.480] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004479)  
for an education where hashtag no one is excluded.

**[03:40:54.960] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004480)  
Mag-i-share ang mga kabataan.

**[03:40:55.360] Multiply-Ed Representative Humphrey Soriano** (asr-004481)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[03:40:58.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004483)  
Next is our Filipino Science National Network,

**[03:41:03.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004484)  
Dr. Elisa Martinez.

**[03:41:05.880] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004485)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[03:41:08.500] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004486)  
Maraming salamat dahil

**[03:41:10.880] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004487)  
tatlo kayo na talagang niloob

**[03:41:13.540] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004488)  
na mag-umpisa na nandito kayo

**[03:41:15.980] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004489)  
para sa briefing na ito.

**[03:41:19.680] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004490)  
At hindi lang kayo nadaan

**[03:41:21.120] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004491)  
at nagpakita

**[03:41:22.680] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004492)  
pero talagang gusto nyo kaming marinig.

**[03:41:25.360] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004493)  
Salamat po sa puwang.

**[03:41:29.440] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004494)  
Tangalan po ng Social Watch Philippines

**[03:41:32.660] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004495)  
na kung hindi dahil sa kanilang budget advocacy

**[03:41:35.860] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004496)  
ay hindi po masasama

**[03:41:37.360] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004497)  
ang mga may kapansanan

**[03:41:38.940] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004498)  
ay ikinatutuwa namin

**[03:41:41.000] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004499)  
na maging bahagi

**[03:41:41.980] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004500)  
ng

**[03:41:43.160] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004501)  
pagbabahagi ng aming mga saloobin.

**[03:41:48.680] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004502)  
Ipagpaumanhin nyo

**[03:41:49.660] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004503)  
dahil ang aming mga puna

**[03:41:51.440] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004504)  
ay hindi lang para sa 2027

**[03:41:53.400] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004505)  
gab.

**[03:41:55.360] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004506)  
Kung hindi, halos ilang dekada na nasama ng loob at hinanakit.

**[03:42:01.360] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004507)  
Kasapi po ang Filipino Sign Language National Network at ang Philippine Alliance of Persons with Chronic Illnesses

**[03:42:07.960] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004508)  
sa Persons with Disabilities Cluster, Social Protection Cluster, Education Cluster, at Health Cluster ng Social Watch.

**[03:42:17.540] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004509)  
Hindi po natin mapagkakaila na lahat tayo ay magkakaroon ng kapansanan at lahat tayo ay tatanda.

**[03:42:24.260] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004510)  
Kaya po, ito po ay isang paksa na malapit sa ating lahat.

**[03:42:30.300] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004511)  
Gusto po namin, Balkan, ang ginawang isang pagsusuri ng Philippine Coalition on the UN Convention on the Rights of Persons with Disabilities

**[03:42:37.940] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004512)  
noong 2013 na bahagi ng Civil Society Report na isinumite sa International Committee ng CRPD.

**[03:42:45.640] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004513)  
Magkano po ba ang ginagastos ng ating pamahalaan sa mga may kapansanan?

**[03:42:52.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004514)  
Ito po ay isang proyekto.

**[03:42:54.260] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004515)  
Ito po ay isang proyekto na isinagawa ng mga may kapansanan mismo mula sa isang pananaw ng karapatang pantao.

**[03:43:02.720] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004516)  
Isa sa mga pinakamahalagang aming nadiskubre at hindi namin alam kung tama ito o meron ng kasunod na ibang aralin,

**[03:43:13.620] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004517)  
maging sa civil society o sa pamahalaan, ay kung magkano nga ba.

**[03:43:18.640] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004518)  
Sa susunod na slide, mapapakita namin ayon sa aming pagsusuri,

**[03:43:24.260] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004519)  
na 0.0952% ng GAA mula sa taong 2012 at 2011 hanggang 2013

**[03:43:36.840] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004520)  
ang ginagastos ng top four national government agencies,

**[03:43:42.000] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004521)  
ang National Council on Disability Affairs, DSWD, DepEd, at DOH.

**[03:43:47.340] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004522)  
Ilang porsyento ng GDP? 0.01825.

**[03:43:54.260] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004523)  
Ilang bang Pilipino ang may mga may kapansanan?

**[03:43:58.120] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004524)  
Ikinatutuwa namin na mula sa 2020 census at 2024 Community-Based Monitoring System, CBMS,

**[03:44:06.740] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004525)  
ay naging mas detalyado na ang pagbilang.

**[03:44:10.980] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004526)  
Sa ngayon, tinatayang 12.9 million Pilipinos with disabilities ang nasa Pilipinas.

**[03:44:20.420] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004527)  
Subalit kung titignan natin ang bilang na ito,

**[03:44:24.260] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004528)  
ang 12.9 million na may kapansanan kailangan doblehin

**[03:44:30.980] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004529)  
kasi kailangang may isang miyembro ng pamamahay na kailangan magbantay o mag-alaga

**[03:44:37.940] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004530)  
at ikinalulukot namin usually ito ay babae, usually ito ay ang ina.

**[03:44:44.720] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004531)  
Kailangan din natin triplehin kasi pag merong may isang may kapansanan

**[03:44:49.440] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004532)  
o kahit isang nakatatanda,

**[03:44:51.700] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004533)  
kailangan pang liban dun sa ina na hindi na makakapagtrabaho,

**[03:44:57.400] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004534)  
makakapaghanap buhay o makakagawa ng iba pa.

**[03:45:00.010] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004535)  
ay meron pang isang kailangang sumalo sa dagdag na gastos ng pamamahay dahil sa kapansanan.

**[03:45:09.900] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004536)  
So effectively, 12.9 million Filipinos with disabilities is equivalent to 36 million

**[03:45:18.500] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004537)  
or one-third of the entire Philippine population impacted by disability, directly or indirectly.

**[03:45:27.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004538)  
What to do? Ito po ang hinihiling namin. Di kami humihingi ng appropriations.

**[03:45:35.140] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004539)  
Ang kailangan namin ay ang pagsik ng Senado kasi talagang bigong-bigo kami sa Kongreso.

**[03:45:42.120] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004540)  
Sa dami ng iba't ibang NGAs na kailangan magpatupad ng laging sinasabing inclusive kami,

**[03:45:50.180] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004541)  
ang sana pong matutukan nyo talaga ay ang PSA.

**[03:45:55.060] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004542)  
Dahil sila yung gumagawa ng censorship.

**[03:45:57.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004543)  
At kasama na rin ang DILG para merong kaming datos.

**[03:46:04.240] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004544)  
Kasi hanggang ngayon, ang sektor ng mga may kapansanan ay lagi ang laban.

**[03:46:10.260] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004545)  
Pag tinanong kami, ilan ba kayo?

**[03:46:15.100] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004546)  
Estimate lang po eh.

**[03:46:17.480] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004547)  
Doon nyo po kami tulungan.

**[03:46:18.980] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004548)  
Dahil pag may bilang, may ebidensya kayo bilang pamahalaan, meron din kaming ebidensya.

**[03:46:26.180] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004549)  
Bilang mga...

**[03:46:27.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004550)  
Mamamayan.

**[03:46:29.740] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004551)  
Pangalawa, sound...

**[03:46:32.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004552)  
Bago ko...

**[03:46:33.280] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004553)  
Bago ko punta doon, yung pong SDGs, isang malaking...

**[03:46:37.060] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004554)  
Isang malaking, very revealing yan.

**[03:46:39.360] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004555)  
Hanggang ngayon, labing liman taon ang binigay para sa SDGs.

**[03:46:44.360] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004556)  
Tatlong taon na lang maglalaps na.

**[03:46:46.860] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004557)  
Alam ba natin ang poverty level ng lahat ng persons with disabilities?

**[03:46:51.420] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004558)  
Lagi natin sinasabi, don't leave no one behind.

**[03:46:54.920] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004559)  
We are already left behind.

**[03:46:57.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004560)  
Children, persons with disabilities, and all the other marginalized sectors.

**[03:47:04.120] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004561)  
What is DEV doing about the SDGs?

**[03:47:08.340] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004562)  
Pangalawa po namin hiling ay ukol sa sound planning.

**[03:47:12.580] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004563)  
Let us be rights-based, updated, responsive.

**[03:47:16.980] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004564)  
I am sorry to say the Magna Carta of Persons with Disabilities,

**[03:47:20.540] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004565)  
Batas Pambansa 344, or the Accessibility Law, is non-CRPD compliant.

**[03:47:27.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004566)  
It is obsolete, and it has never been updated.

**[03:47:32.720] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004567)  
Let us do something about it.

**[03:47:35.960] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004568)  
Bakit hanggang ngayon, nagkaroon na ng Filipino Sign Language Act

**[03:47:39.560] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004569)  
na kinilala ang Filipino Sign Language bilang pambansang wikang senyas,

**[03:47:45.280] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004570)  
eh ang pinaglalaban pa rin madalas bukang bibig sa DepEd ay American Sign Language?

**[03:47:51.180] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004571)  
Bakit sa RA 12027 na isinabatas itong...

**[03:47:57.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004572)  
Last year lang, tinanggal ang mother tongue.

**[03:48:01.540] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004573)  
Tinamaan lahat ng tatutubo, pati na ang deaf kasama.

**[03:48:07.040] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004574)  
So susunod na slide.

**[03:48:11.400] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004575)  
Dito po kailangan nyo ibigay na.

**[03:48:14.100] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004576)

**[03:48:17.220] Filipino Sign Language National Network Convenor Dr. Liza B. Martinez** (asr-004577)

**[03:56:48.280] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004771)

million for the HIV

**[03:57:57.540] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004806)  
and Adolescent Pregnancy Capacity Development

Program of DepEd, a youth

**[03:58:02.200] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004808)

**[03:58:12.240] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004813)

**[03:58:14.720] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004814)

**[03:58:16.940] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004815)

**[03:58:18.200] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004816)  
does not encourage them,

**[03:58:20.220] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004817)

**[03:58:22.640] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004818)  
The society have been telling young

**[03:58:24.160] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004819)

**[03:58:26.140] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004820)

**[03:58:28.040] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004821)

**[03:58:30.040] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004822)  
cases among young people.

**[03:58:32.080] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004823)

**[03:58:34.660] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004824)  
What we can control is the right

**[03:58:36.060] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004825)

**[03:58:38.300] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004826)

**[03:58:40.100] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004827)  
By doing so, we are equipping young people

**[03:58:42.300] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004828)

**[03:58:44.460] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004829)

**[03:58:46.520] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004830)  
Kaya sa mga tao po na patuloy

**[03:58:48.100] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004831)  
na nagsasabing ilayo sa mga kabataan,

**[03:58:50.220] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004832)

**[03:58:54.240] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004834)

**[03:58:56.340] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004835)  
Sa akin pong pagtatapos,

**[03:58:57.980] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004836)  
ang HIV response ay hindi

**[03:59:00.180] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004837)

That's why other agencies

**[03:59:06.120] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004840)

**[03:59:08.460] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004841)

**[03:59:09.860] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004842)  
para mas ma-achieve po natin

ang ating 95-95-95 targets

**[03:59:14.380] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004844)

HIV epidemic sa ating bansa

**[03:59:17.960] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004846)  
at pati na rin ang tumataas sa gender-based,

**[03:59:20.220] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004847)  
discrimination.

**[03:59:21.580] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004848)  
Inaasahan po natin ang Senado para sa mas masaya,

**[03:59:24.220] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004849)  
makulay at maunlad at pantay

**[03:59:26.200] Pantay Representative Airon Banyapando** (asr-004850)

**[04:00:12.860] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004869)

**[04:00:15.140] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004870)  
But we'll get to that later.

**[04:00:16.380] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004871)  
Next slide, please.

**[04:00:17.580] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004872)  
So, the People's Budget Coalition already mentioned this earlier,

**[04:00:20.400] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004873)  
so I'm just going to get straight to the point here.

**[04:00:22.420] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004874)  
What I would like to show here instead is a needs-based visualization

**[04:00:25.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004875)  
of one of the DPWH infrastructure project types

**[04:00:30.500] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004876)  
compared to the drought projections of Pag-Asa itself

**[04:00:34.520] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004877)  
that they shared a couple of weeks ago only.

**[04:00:36.820] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004878)  
This is the October latest version of Pag-Asa's drought.

**[04:00:39.660] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004879)  
Kitang-kita naman po, mas kulay blue,

**[04:00:42.880] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004880)  
mas mataas ang budget allocation.

**[04:00:44.920] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004881)  
Ito po ay galing sa HGAB.

**[04:00:47.400] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004882)  
Mas red, mas malakas ang chance ang magkakadroughts.

**[04:00:50.780] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004883)  
By March 2027, wala po tayong takas.

**[04:00:53.680] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004884)  
Every single province will be,

**[04:00:55.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004885)  
suffering droughts.

**[04:00:57.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004886)  
Pinapakita po natin yung October 2026

**[04:00:59.500] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004887)  
kahit di po talaga siya sakot palagan ng 2027 budget

**[04:01:01.980] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004888)  
dahil ito pong mga pabinsya na ito na ating minakahan sa bilog,

**[04:01:05.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004889)  
ito po ang mga unang tatamaan ng droughts.

**[04:01:07.420] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004890)  
At tinatamaan na po sila ng droughts.

**[04:01:09.280] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004891)  
Bicol, Eastern Visayas.

**[04:01:11.600] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004892)  
Sila po ba ang dapat makuha ng budget,

**[04:01:15.240] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004893)  
prioritization-wise, sa water infrastructure?

**[04:01:18.100] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004894)  
Ipa pa pong irrigation dito.

**[04:01:19.340] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004895)  
This is just water infrastructure projects of DPWH.

**[04:01:21.480] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004896)  
Rainwater harvesting and solar water pumps, among others.

**[04:01:25.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004897)  
Pero kung tina po natin yung January 2027,

**[04:01:27.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004898)  
Western Visayas, Mindoro, sila rin po ang ilang unang tatamaan ng droughts.

**[04:01:32.480] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004899)  
But are they getting the biggest cut of the water infrastructure project of DPWH?

**[04:01:37.380] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004900)  
No.

**[04:01:38.380] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004901)  
So, this is another example of the needs-based assessment

**[04:01:42.000] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004902)  
na kailangan gawin sa lahat ng proyekta ng gobyerno.

**[04:01:45.200] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004903)  
Ito po ay nasa Section 40 ng GAAM mismo ng 2026

**[04:01:49.100] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004904)  
and it's even in the NEP.

**[04:01:50.220] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004905)  
All government projects, multi-probabilistic assessment.

**[04:01:54.600] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004906)  
Dapat may ganun po, needs-based.

**[04:01:57.760] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004907)  
Nasa GAAM po yan at nasa NEP.

**[04:01:59.900] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004908)  
So, ito sa ipotong halimbawa ng needs-based assessment na kailangan gawin.

**[04:02:03.080] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004909)  
Next slide, please.

**[04:02:05.360] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004910)  
Here's another interesting thing that I'm honestly surprised has not been mentioned as much so far.

**[04:02:10.840] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004911)  
Let's talk about nature-based solutions.

**[04:02:12.640] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004912)  
One of the newest buzzwords when it comes to the climate and environment sector in this country.

**[04:02:17.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004913)  
Alam naman po natin na kulang na kulang po sa pondo.

**[04:02:20.520] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004914)  
So, what I would try to do instead is give you numbers.

**[04:02:23.700] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004915)  
Last year,

**[04:02:24.600] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004916)  
wala pa pong kongkretong estimate ng nature-based solutions funding sa Pilipinas.

**[04:02:29.560] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004917)  
So, the closest for us in action climate that we got

**[04:02:31.740] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004918)  
is NCCAP or National Climate Change Action Plan Goal No. 3

**[04:02:35.560] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004919)  
which corresponds to ecological and environmental stability.

**[04:02:39.260] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004920)  
All environmental projects are under Goal 3 of NCCAP.

**[04:02:43.740] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004921)  
It's only 0.12% of this year's GAAM.

**[04:02:46.980] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004922)  
Ang ASEAN public expenditures percentage for nature-based solutions, 0.64%.

**[04:02:52.760] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004923)  
If we do the math,

**[04:02:53.680] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004924)  
ibig sabihin po niyan,

**[04:02:55.920] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004925)  
8 billion for 2026 GAAM ang nature-based solutions,

**[04:02:58.480] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004926)  
well, scale-wise,

**[04:02:59.880] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004927)  
kasi wala pa pong kongkretong estimate,

**[04:03:01.380] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004928)  
pero ang ASEAN public expenditures for nature-based solutions,

**[04:03:05.560] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004929)  
43 billion ang katumbas niya sa GAAM.

**[04:03:08.200] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004930)  
And if we apply it to this year's NEP,

**[04:03:10.220] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004931)  
7.2 billion,

**[04:03:11.480] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004932)  
that would be 46 billion.

**[04:03:13.840] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004933)  
Sinabi po ni Yusek Anate na ang kasunod ko yung budget po ng DNR,

**[04:03:18.260] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004934)  
1.7 billion lamang.

**[04:03:20.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004935)  
That's a lot of money still,

**[04:03:22.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004936)  
but that's not enough.

**[04:03:24.220] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004937)  
And that's just DNR.

**[04:03:25.560] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004938)  
Pero yung scale po nung 0.12% versus 0.64%,

**[04:03:29.340] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004939)  
1.7 billion versus 46 billion,

**[04:03:32.180] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004940)  
simply not enough.

**[04:03:36.460] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004941)  
Kung kaya po ng camera na magmanipulan ng daang-daang bilyong piso para sa infra-projects,

**[04:03:40.980] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004942)  
what is stopping anyone from allotting more to nature-based solutions?

**[04:03:45.760] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004943)  
Next slide, please.

**[04:03:47.480] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004944)  
And that's why to our esteemed senators,

**[04:03:49.220] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004945)  
we'd like to forward the following recommendations.

**[04:03:50.560] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004946)  
To further super El Nino-proof our nationalization,

**[04:03:53.680] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004947)  
our national budget for next year.

**[04:03:55.260] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004948)  
Ilan po dito ay nabanggit na po nila Sen. Gatchelyan at Sen. Ejercito in the last couple of days.

**[04:04:00.340] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004949)  
So we're just reiterating.

**[04:04:02.060] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004950)  
PCIC, bakit babawasan ang cap insurance?

**[04:04:05.040] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004951)  
It doesn't make any sense.

**[04:04:06.180] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004952)  
Bakit babawasan ang NRIMPA?

**[04:04:07.540] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004953)  
Nabanggit na po yan kanina ng Code NGO.

**[04:04:10.560] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004954)  
So we're not going to go much into that.

**[04:04:12.500] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004955)  
You should not.

**[04:04:13.680] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004956)  
Ang kailangan po nating makita ay ang Super El Nino National Action Plan.

**[04:04:17.060] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004957)  
That is what should be the basis for a needs-based assessment.

**[04:04:23.020] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004958)  
PSF.

**[04:04:23.680] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004959)  
I think may opportunity po tayo na i-examine ang disbursement ng PSF.

**[04:04:30.240] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004960)  
Makakatulong po ang PSF sa adaptation lalapos Super El Nino.

**[04:04:35.500] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004961)  
And our proposal for nature-based solutions,

**[04:04:39.060] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004962)  
1.7 billion for DNR right now in the NEP.

**[04:04:42.140] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004963)  
Why not 1 billion per region?

**[04:04:44.240] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004964)  
Transboundary nature-based solutions.

**[04:04:46.160] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004965)  
More communities will benefit.

**[04:04:47.500] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004966)  
We invest in nature-based solutions.

**[04:04:49.580] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004967)  
We invest in our future.

**[04:04:52.200] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004968)  
And that's why for our final slide,

**[04:04:53.680] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004969)  
as promised,

**[04:04:56.340] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004970)  
we would like to share to our esteemed senators

**[04:04:58.880] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004971)  
and to everyone and to the dozens watching on Facebook Live

**[04:05:02.160] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004972)  
our two Philippine Climate Budget Reports.

**[04:05:06.020] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004973)  
We actually did a report last year po,

**[04:05:07.700] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004974)  
which examines 2020 to 2025.

**[04:05:11.300] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004975)  
But moving forward,

**[04:05:12.420] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004976)  
we will start an annual examination

**[04:05:14.660] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004977)  
of the climate tagging system in the Philippines.

**[04:05:17.480] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004978)  
For the record,

**[04:05:18.160] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004979)

**[04:05:19.760] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004980)

**[04:05:20.840] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004981)  
CCC po yan at DBM.

**[04:05:22.200] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004982)  
But ang nagtatagpo ng program,

**[04:05:23.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004983)  
projects,

**[04:05:24.200] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004984)  
ay hindi CCC at DBM lang.

**[04:05:25.820] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004985)  
Every agency tags their own projects.

**[04:05:28.240] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004986)  
So we'd just like to share these reports.

**[04:05:30.080] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004987)  
Make your record po.

**[04:05:30.920] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004988)  
And we have shared copies online,

**[04:05:33.420] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004989)  
also to the committee,

**[04:05:34.740] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004990)

**[04:05:35.720] Aksyon Klima Pilipinas National Coordinator John Leo Algo** (asr-004991)

**[04:05:49.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-004999)

**[04:05:50.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005000)  
I told,

**[04:05:51.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005001)  
we recommended,

**[04:05:53.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005002)  
no?

**[04:05:53.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005003)  
That,

**[04:05:53.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005004)  
instead na mag-focus sila sa flood control projects,

**[04:05:59.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005005)

**[04:06:01.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005006)

**[04:06:04.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005007)

**[04:06:06.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005008)  
diba?

**[04:06:07.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005009)  
Dapat water retention,

**[04:06:09.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005010)  
impounding,

**[04:06:11.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005011)  
because we have so much water every rainy season,

**[04:06:13.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005012)  
and pag dry season,

**[04:06:15.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005013)

**[04:06:16.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005014)

**[04:06:18.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005015)  
So we'll be hooting two birds with one stone,

**[04:06:21.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005016)  
kung ganun sana,

**[04:06:22.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005017)  
na,

**[04:06:23.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005018)  
mapabagal natin yung takbo ng tubig,

**[04:06:29.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005019)  
no?

**[04:06:30.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005020)  
Cascading.

**[04:06:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005021)  
Tapos at the same time,

**[04:06:32.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005022)  
we save water,

**[04:06:33.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005023)  
diba?

**[04:06:33.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005024)  
On dry days.

**[04:06:35.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005025)  
Nangyayari sa flood control,

**[04:06:36.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005026)

**[04:06:39.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005027)  
ano,

**[04:06:40.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005028)  
diba?

**[04:06:40.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005029)  
River banks,

**[04:06:41.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005030)  
lahat.

**[04:06:42.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005031)  
Buminis yung takbo ng tubig,

**[04:06:43.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005032)  
hindi pa nare-replenish yung groundwater,

**[04:06:46.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005033)

**[04:06:46.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005034)  
diba?

**[04:06:47.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005035)  
So,

**[04:06:48.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005036)  
we'll see.

**[04:06:48.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005037)  
I'm hoping,

**[04:06:49.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005038)

**[04:18:22.320] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005473)

**[04:18:24.540] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005474)

**[04:18:32.760] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005475)  
Ngayon, ang tanong po, sino po ang in-charge sa road safety sa Pilipinas?

**[04:18:37.860] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005476)  
Per administrative order po ni PGMA, dahil binuwag po niya yung National Road Safety Committee, which was a cabinet-level committee meeting every so often on road safety,

**[04:18:49.080] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005477)  
ang nangyayari po ngayon ay si DOTR ang may responsibility sa road safety.

**[04:18:53.720] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005478)

**[04:18:54.520] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005479)

**[04:19:03.780] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005480)  
Ngayon, naulit na po, since he's now Transportation Secretary, yung problema na po dito, ang mga naka-assign na tao for Task Force Road Safety, busy rin po sila sa Public Transport Modernization Program.

**[04:19:17.560] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005481)

**[04:19:21.240] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005482)  
Which is why, and DOTR,

**[04:19:24.520] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005483)  
marvellous document called the PRSAP, the Philippine Road Safety Action Plan, 2023 to 2028, eh 2026 na po.

**[04:19:32.200] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005484)

**[04:19:39.660] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005485)

**[04:19:50.260] Move As One Coalition Road Safety Advocate Lou Hepuela** (asr-005486)  
please, please fund road safety. Thank you po.

**[04:28:28.180] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005667)

**[04:28:32.760] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005668)

**[04:28:34.700] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005669)

**[04:28:38.480] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005670)

**[04:28:41.100] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005671)

**[04:28:44.560] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005672)  
Of that 7.2 trillion proposed budget of the 2027 General Appropriations Act or GAAA,

**[04:28:50.920] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005673)

**[04:28:54.360] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005674)

**[04:28:59.240] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005675)  
Hence, the imbalanced allocation of budget

**[04:29:02.620] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005676)  
has failed to accommodate opportunities

**[04:29:05.260] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005677)  
for cultural heritage conservation.

**[04:29:08.420] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005678)  
In doing so,

**[04:29:09.640] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005679)  
the tourism and cultural heritage sector

**[04:29:12.000] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005680)  
remained underfunded and understaffed.

**[04:29:15.620] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005681)  
One government agency could not efficiently address

**[04:29:18.460] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005682)  
the numerous cost-efficient restoration

It is clear that such imposes challenges on culture and heritage, such as vulnerabilities to deterioration, disaster risk management,

**[04:31:48.240] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005725)

NCCA, National Commission.

for Culture and the Arts, was only given a budget of 152 million pesos, which is 2 million pesos less than the Intramuros Administration,

**[04:34:54.420] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005748)  
Such investments can preserve irreplaceable cultural heritage.

**[04:34:58.620] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005750)  
While creating opportunities for public services, local employment, commercial activity, tourism, and community-oriented urban renewal.

**[04:35:10.360] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005751)  
And can be concretely repurposed as museums, libraries, cultural centers, kahit nga po health center at housing.

**[04:35:43.840] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005758)

**[04:35:46.160] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005759)

**[04:35:52.140] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005760)

**[04:35:57.380] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005761)  
existing assets, useful, accessible, and economically productive.

**[04:36:02.560] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005762)

**[04:36:09.800] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005763)

**[04:36:18.460] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005764)

**[04:36:26.600] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005765)  
investments can stimulate local economies, create livelihoods, and attract continued economic activity,

**[04:36:34.540] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005766)

**[04:36:46.600] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005768)  
With sustained budgetary support, these benefits can extend beyond immediate tourism revenues to deliver long-term returns.

**[04:36:56.600] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005769)

**[04:37:05.820] Renacimiento Manila Representative Bea Dolores** (asr-005770)

Public Services Labor Independent Confederation or PSLINK, Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron.

**[04:39:42.380] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005799)

**[04:39:45.780] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005800)

**[04:39:52.520] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005801)

**[04:39:56.600] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005802)

**[04:40:05.660] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005803)

**[04:40:09.660] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005804)

**[04:40:21.820] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005805)

**[04:40:24.700] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005806)

**[04:40:36.560] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005807)

**[04:40:47.440] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005808)

**[04:40:49.840] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005809)

**[04:40:54.700] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005810)

**[04:41:07.360] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005811)

**[04:41:18.220] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005812)

**[04:41:24.700] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005813)

**[04:41:35.040] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005814)

**[04:41:50.860] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005816)

**[04:41:59.320] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-005818)

**[04:50:49.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006021)

**[04:50:53.720] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006023)

**[04:50:55.760] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006024)

**[04:50:57.960] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006025)

**[04:50:59.980] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006026)

**[04:51:02.700] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006027)

**[04:51:03.840] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006028)

**[04:51:04.920] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006029)

**[04:51:08.460] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006030)

**[04:51:10.260] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006031)

**[04:51:11.820] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006032)

**[04:51:13.760] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006033)

**[04:51:15.940] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006034)

**[04:51:17.880] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006035)

**[04:51:19.620] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006036)

**[04:51:21.460] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006037)

**[04:51:23.760] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006038)
