**[04:51:26.320] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006039)  
Justisya naman po para

**[04:51:27.800] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006040)

**[04:51:29.820] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006041)

**[04:51:31.880] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006042)

**[04:51:34.020] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006043)

**[04:51:35.900] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006044)

**[04:51:37.800] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006045)

**[04:51:40.520] PSLINK President Dr. Annie Enriquez-Geron** (asr-006046)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:51:41.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006047)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:51:43.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006048)

**[04:51:46.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006049)

**[04:51:47.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006050)

**[04:51:49.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006051)

**[04:51:51.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006052)

**[04:51:57.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006055)

**[04:51:59.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006056)

**[04:52:01.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006057)

**[04:52:03.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006058)

**[04:52:05.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006059)

**[04:52:10.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006060)  
Sige po, before we continue,

**[04:52:11.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006061)

**[04:52:13.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006062)

**[04:52:16.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006063)  
or questions.

**[04:52:17.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006064)  
Mr. Chairman, sa ating mga

**[04:52:19.280] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006065)

**[04:52:21.500] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006066)

**[04:52:23.540] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006067)

**[04:52:25.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006068)

**[04:52:27.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006069)

**[04:52:29.520] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006070)

**[04:52:32.520] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006071)

**[04:52:33.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006072)

**[04:52:35.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006073)

**[04:52:38.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006074)

**[04:52:39.500] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006075)

**[04:52:41.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006076)

**[04:52:43.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006077)

**[04:52:45.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006078)  
side of the coin.

**[04:52:47.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006079)  
By the way, yung sinabi ko

**[04:52:49.760] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006080)

**[04:52:52.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006081)

**[04:52:53.900] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006082)  
Kasi maraming salamat,

**[04:52:55.540] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006083)

**[04:52:57.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006084)

**[04:53:00.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006085)  
at saka nakakapagpalakas,

**[04:53:01.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006086)  
nakapag-strengthen ang resolve.

**[04:53:03.540] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006087)  
So kami, malakas yung aming

**[04:53:05.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006088)

**[04:53:07.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006089)

**[04:53:10.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006090)  
Pero ngayon, nadagdagan pa yung

**[04:53:11.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006091)  
resolve namin after hearing all of you.

**[04:53:13.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006092)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:53:15.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006093)  
Thank you.

**[04:53:17.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006094)  
I agree with Senator Lacson. Minsan,

**[04:53:19.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006095)  
sa dami ng problema, sa manarinig namin

**[04:53:21.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006096)  
mga issues ngayon,

**[04:53:23.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006097)  
humihirap na mali ng Pilipinas.

**[04:53:25.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006098)  
But on the other hand,

**[04:53:26.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006099)  
sabi nga niya,

**[04:53:27.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006100)  
ni Senator Lacson,

**[04:53:29.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006101)  
pag naririnig naman yung passion nyo,

**[04:53:31.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006102)  
yung talagang dedication nyo,

**[04:53:33.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006103)  
syempre, masarap na lumaban pa rin.

**[04:53:36.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006104)  
Laban lang tayo.

**[04:53:37.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006105)  
Kahit mahirap. Minsan,

**[04:53:39.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006106)  
ang ating challenge.

**[04:53:41.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-006107)  
Kung sila na kaya mag-takeover ng gobyerno.

**[04:53:45.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006108)  
O nga, para makapag-relax na.

**[04:53:47.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006109)  
Mahirap din sa gobyerno.

**[04:53:48.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006110)  
Pag yung mga nagtatrabaho talaga,

**[04:53:50.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006111)  
yung pang nababatikos.

**[04:53:52.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006112)  
Kaya laban lang tayo.

**[04:53:54.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006113)  
Meron lang konti katanungan.

**[04:53:56.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006114)  
Anybody can answer?

**[04:53:57.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006115)  
Before we adjourn today.

**[04:54:01.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006116)  
Yun sa ilang beses nabanggit kasi kanina

**[04:54:04.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006117)  
tungkol sa ayuda.

**[04:54:06.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006118)  
Siguro, pwede sila Kenneth or si AJ.

**[04:54:11.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006119)  
Or any.

**[04:54:12.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006120)  
Si Dr. Raquiza.

**[04:54:13.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006121)  
Wala na si Manibig.

**[04:54:14.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006122)  
Do we have any study on the economic impact

**[04:54:17.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006123)  
of the financial assistance or ayuda?

**[04:54:20.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006124)  
For example, for every billion spent on ayuda,

**[04:54:23.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006125)  
how much economic activity does it actually generate?

**[04:54:26.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006126)  
Meron ba tayong ganung study?

**[04:54:27.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006127)  
Whenever we ask agencies to justify their budgets for financial assistance,

**[04:54:33.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006128)  
they always emphasize the importance of ayuda.

**[04:54:36.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006129)  
Especially with the ongoing oil crisis and the threat of super-L-linio.

**[04:54:40.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006130)  
Meron ba tayong ganun study sa economic impact or financial assistance of yung ayuda?

**[04:54:47.480] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006131)  
Salamat po, Chair JV.

**[04:54:49.440] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006132)  
To answer it very quickly, we don't have good studies po.

**[04:54:53.640] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006133)  
Ang merong nag-aaral po on this subject is yung PIDS po.

**[04:54:57.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006134)  
Yung Philippine Institute for Development Studies.

**[04:54:59.360] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006135)  
They're currently doing studies on the MAIFIP on the AICS.

**[04:55:02.480] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006136)  
Medyo nakapagtataka kung bakit ngayon lang po ito nangyari

**[04:55:05.720] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006137)  
because we've been adding more and more money.

**[04:55:08.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006138)  
Our colleagues from the health sector, they've done some studies on the MAIFIP.

**[04:55:12.360] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006139)  
Ang nakita po nila ay karamihan po ng MAIFIP funds

**[04:55:14.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006140)  
ay nagpupunta sa mga middle or upper class na patients.

**[04:55:18.920] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006141)  
Hindi po siya nagpupunta sa mga mahihirap na pasyente.

**[04:55:21.900] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006142)  
On the other hand, yung mga rules-based social protection like yung 4Ps,

**[04:55:25.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006143)  
marami pong mga international studies,

**[04:55:27.320] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006144)  
that really show how effective po yung 4Ps is.

**[04:55:30.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006145)  
So that's really what we're advocating for,

**[04:55:32.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006146)  
na ilipat po yung pondo away from itong mga discretionary AICs

**[04:55:36.060] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006147)  
at mas dagdagan at suportahan po natin yung 4Ps na program.

**[04:55:40.520] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006148)  
Maybe I can help you.

**[04:55:41.660] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Adolfo Jose Montesa** (asr-006149)  
Hi, si Ken po.

**[04:55:42.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006150)  
Sige.

**[04:55:44.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006151)  
Yes, si...

**[04:55:46.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006152)  
Sino ba niya?

**[04:55:48.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006153)  
Ken.

**[04:55:49.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006154)  
Ken Abante.

**[04:55:50.760] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006155)  
We can send you a copy of this study, Senator JV.

**[04:55:53.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006156)  
There was a recent World Bank study that talked about cost-effectiveness

**[04:55:56.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006157)  
of different social protection programs,

**[04:55:58.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006158)  
of which yung 4Ps nga po was the most effective of all the social protection programs.

**[04:56:04.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006159)  
Incidentally, in that same chart,

**[04:56:06.040] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006160)  
you can also see how less effective the other programs are,

**[04:56:11.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006161)  
including MAIFIP and AICs.

**[04:56:13.100] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006162)  
So may kita niyo po doon yung current state ng evidence,

**[04:56:16.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006163)  
at least there.

**[04:56:17.500] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006164)  
I will send you and your staff a copy of the study.

**[04:56:19.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-006165)  
Thank you, Sen.

**[04:56:20.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006166)  
Thank you for that.

**[04:56:21.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006167)  
We will need that siguro pa if you can send this committee.

**[04:56:26.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006168)  
Actually, dito sa nakita namin,

**[04:56:29.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006169)  
nag-uusap namin sa Senator Lacson,

**[04:56:31.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006170)  
Sen. President Gachalian,

**[04:56:34.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006171)  
although admittedly, we still need to have those social programs.

**[04:56:40.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006172)  
Dahil nga may El Niño tayo,

**[04:56:41.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006173)  
meron tayong crisis,

**[04:56:42.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006174)  
pero ang sa akin lang,

**[04:56:44.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006175)  
personally, sa akin po ito,

**[04:56:46.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006176)  
huwag naman lahat po sa ayuda.

**[04:56:47.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006177)  
Hindi naman bumuhay.

**[04:56:48.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006178)  
Aasa tayo sa ayuda eh.

**[04:56:50.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006179)  
Di ba?

**[04:56:50.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006180)  
This is not sustainable.

**[04:56:52.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006181)  
Doon pa rin po ako sa...

**[04:56:53.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006182)  
Sana kung mabalansin natin,

**[04:56:55.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006183)  
huwag natin kalimutan yung mga legacy projects.

**[04:57:00.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006184)  
Lalo na yung mga...

**[04:57:00.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006185)  
Those that will really stimulate economic growth.

**[04:57:04.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006186)  
Lalo na yung magpapasiglan ng ekonomi natin in the future.

**[04:57:09.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006187)  
Kaya doon pa rin ako sa infra.

**[04:57:11.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006188)  
Talagang hindi natin pwedeng pabaya.

**[04:57:13.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006189)  
It may cost, yes, definitely,

**[04:57:14.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006190)  
but the returns to the economy will be enormous in the future.

**[04:57:19.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006191)  
Di ba?

**[04:57:19.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006192)  
Hindi naman ito rocket science.

**[04:57:21.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006193)  
Yan ang ginawa ng ASEAN neighbors natin.

**[04:57:24.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006194)  
Di ba?

**[04:57:25.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006195)  
Malaysia,

**[04:59:40.580] Healthcare Professional Alliance Representative Dr. Albert Ong** (asr-006239)

**[05:17:26.820] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007057)  
Thank you, thank you po.

**[05:17:27.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007058)  
Just on the local government support fund, Chair JV.

**[05:17:31.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007059)  
Actually, maganda po siguro yung i-distinguish yung ibang components

**[05:17:33.780] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007060)  
ng local government support fund.

**[05:17:35.280] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007061)  
Because there are three major funds under the LGSF.

**[05:17:38.120] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007062)  
Under the HGAB, around 62 billion na siya.

**[05:17:40.680] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007063)  
Meron pong financial assistance to LGUs.

**[05:17:42.740] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007064)  
Actually, yun po yung pinag-uusapan natin kanina, 41.5 billion.

**[05:17:46.220] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007065)  
There's also a second category called the growth equity fund.

**[05:17:49.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007066)  
Around 10.3 billion.

**[05:17:50.600] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007067)  
This is supposed to change the inequitable allocations

**[05:17:53.840] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007068)  
by the national tax allotment.

**[05:17:56.340] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007069)  
Although we know that at the longer term,

**[05:17:59.200] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007070)  
yung kailangan po talaga natin ay a change in the way that NTA is computed.

**[05:18:02.620] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007071)  
Which I think there are pending bills also pushed by Local Gov PH,

**[05:18:06.300] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007072)  
one of our coalition members.

**[05:18:08.160] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007073)  
And then, third po, si NTF-ELCAC support to barangay is 9.7 billion.

**[05:18:12.720] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007074)  
Which we're all saying we should abolish it and just remove the funding for that.

**[05:18:17.480] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007075)  
So that we can increase funding for

**[05:18:20.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007076)  
for the improvement in the equity in the changes.

**[05:18:26.000] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007077)  
So ngayon naman po, sa financial assistance to LGUs in particular,

**[05:18:29.260] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007078)  
yung iba pong mga iniisip namin na designs include a matching fund.

**[05:18:34.700] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007079)  
Yung matching fund ng LGUs.

**[05:18:36.980] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007080)  
Kasi for example, dapat po merong nationally mandated programs

**[05:18:43.020] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007081)  
such as for example, malnutrition programs na pwedeng nakailangan,

**[05:18:47.400] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007082)  
na priorities talaga for early childhood.

**[05:18:50.240] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007083)  
At 1,000 days, the local government units will only get the fund

**[05:18:54.560] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007084)  
if they provide counterpart funding para doble yung financial assistance.

**[05:18:59.440] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007085)  
We can also do the same for mobility, for local transport and mobility offices.

**[05:19:03.460] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007086)  
So maybe we can think of a special provision and we can design something together as NJV

**[05:19:08.980] People's Budget Coalition Co-convenor Kenneth Isaiah Ibasco Abante** (asr-007087)  
na maging policy-based yung financial assistance to LGUs at hindi siya magbukang pork barrel.

**[05:19:14.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007088)  
Thank you for that.

**[05:19:15.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007089)

**[05:19:56.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007098)

**[05:19:59.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007099)  
Ganun din, di ba?

**[05:20:01.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007100)

**[05:20:05.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007101)

**[05:20:15.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007102)  
Okay, we'll wait for that.

**[05:20:17.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007103)  
We will, ano, sa aming committee amendment.
